# GPT: Use this file to add your file changes.

## 🔧 INSTRUCTIONS

You are a developer. You will receive file paths and contents inside this single document.
You must edit the contents of the files according to the prompt **without altering the file structure**.

## 📌 DEFINITIONS
- "FILE_SEPARATOR": A line that separates each file section.
  -->FILE_SEPARATOR<--

- "Path --> --> [file path]": Indicates the start of a new file block.

## ✅ RULES

1. File sections are separated by '-->FILE_SEPARATOR<--'.
2. Each section starts with "Path --> --> [absolute or relative path]".
3. After that line comes the full content of the file.
4. Only edit **the contents** of existing files. **Do not change file paths.**
5. To create a new file, add a new section with "-->FILE_SEPARATOR<--" and "Path -->" --> [new path]".
6. Never delete or skip file sections.
7. Do not modify or remove  "-->FILE_SEPARATOR<--" or "Path -->" markers.
8. Preserve formatting, indentation, and structure exactly.
9. When replying, always return the full updated structure with all sections.

## 📎 EXAMPLE STRUCTURE

-->FILE_SEPARATOR<--
Path --> /index.html
<html>
  <body>Hello</body>
</html>

-->FILE_SEPARATOR<--
Path --> /script.js
console.log("Hello, world!");

import { chmod, mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { autoDirectorPromptVersion } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/auto-director.mjs';
import { getReadyIterations } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/exhibition-readiness.mjs';

const assemblyScript = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../eu-reski-news/scripts/assemble-news-film.mjs', import.meta.url));

describe('single-scene Auto exhibition assembly', () => {
  test('assembles one Auto scene with existing-bridges mode and emits no glue boundary', async () => {
    const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-single-assembly-'));
    const runDirectory = join(root, 'run');
    const glueDirectory = join(root, 'glue');
    const iterationDirectory = join(runDirectory, 'iteration-001');
    const mediaDirectory = join(iterationDirectory, 'media');
    const followingDirectory = join(runDirectory, 'iteration-002');
    const followingMediaDirectory = join(followingDirectory, 'media');
    const fakeFfmpeg = join(root, 'fake-ffmpeg.cjs');
    try {
      await mkdir(mediaDirectory, { recursive: true });
      await mkdir(followingMediaDirectory, { recursive: true });
      await mkdir(glueDirectory, { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(join(mediaDirectory, 'scene-1-video.mp4'), 'fixture media');
      await writeFile(join(iterationDirectory, 'record.json'), JSON.stringify({
        status: 'Abgeschlossen',
        autoMode: true,
        autoDecision: { promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion },
        sceneCount: 1,
        plan: { scenes: [{ index: 1 }] },
      }));
      await writeFile(join(followingMediaDirectory, 'scene-1-video.mp4'), 'fixture media');
      await writeFile(join(followingMediaDirectory, 'scene-2-video.mp4'), 'fixture media');
      await writeFile(join(followingDirectory, 'record.json'), JSON.stringify({
        status: 'Abgeschlossen',
        sceneCount: 2,
        plan: { scenes: [{ index: 1 }, { index: 2 }] },
      }));
      await writeFile(join(runDirectory, 'run.json'), JSON.stringify({
        iterations: [
          { index: 1, directory: 'iteration-001' },
          { index: 2, directory: 'iteration-002' },
        ],
      }));
      await writeFile(fakeFfmpeg, [
        '#!/usr/bin/env node',
        "const fs = require('node:fs');",
        "const args = process.argv.slice(2);",
        "const calls = fs.existsSync(process.env.FAKE_FFMPEG_ARGS) ? JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.env.FAKE_FFMPEG_ARGS, 'utf8')) : [];",
        "calls.push(args); fs.writeFileSync(process.env.FAKE_FFMPEG_ARGS, JSON.stringify(calls));",
        "fs.writeFileSync(args.at(-1), 'assembled fixture');",
      ].join('\n'));
      await chmod(fakeFfmpeg, 0o755);

      const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [
        assemblyScript,
        `--run-dir=${runDirectory}`,
        `--scene-glue-dir=${glueDirectory}`,
        `--limit=${(await getReadyIterations(runDirectory)).length}`,
        '--existing-bridges-only',
        '--no-generate-title-cards',
      ], {
        encoding: 'utf8',
        env: { ...process.env, FFMPEG_BIN: fakeFfmpeg, FAKE_FFMPEG_ARGS: join(root, 'ffmpeg-args.json') },
      });
      expect(await getReadyIterations(runDirectory)).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(result.status).toBe(0);
      const manifest = await readFile(join(glueDirectory, 'film-order.txt'), 'utf8');
      expect(manifest).toContain('iteration-001/media/scene-1-video.mp4');
      expect(manifest).not.toContain('scene-001-to-');
      expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(root, 'ffmpeg-args.json'), 'utf8')).flat())
        .toContain(join(glueDirectory, 'eu-reski-first-2-glued.mp4'));
      expect(await readFile(join(glueDirectory, 'eu-reski-first-2-glued.mp4'), 'utf8')).toBe('assembled fixture');
    } finally {
      await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });
});

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/auto-director.test.js
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import {
  autoDirectorPromptVersion,
  autoDirectorSchema,
  buildAutoDirectorRequest,
  resolveAutoModeSetting,
  resolveAutoDecisionAction,
  resolveEffectiveAutoFlags,
  resolveSavedAutoDecision,
  validateAutoDirectorResponse,
} from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/auto-director.mjs';

const flags = (overrides = {}) => ({
  storyClou: 'off',
  legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: false,
  preserveCubistVideoStyle: true,
  sceneVisualControls: 'scene-specific',
  articleImageReference: true,
  landscapePrompt: false,
  videoDirection: 'on',
  videoVision: 'off',
  videoAudioDirection: 'off',
  videoMaxZoom: 0.08,
  ...overrides,
});

const decision = (selected = flags(), { iteration = 1, articleId = 'article-1', constraints = {} } = {}) => {
  const resolved = resolveEffectiveAutoFlags(selected, constraints);
  return {
    autoMode: true,
    iteration,
    articleId,
    model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
    promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion,
    dramaturgicalConcept: 'An open, fragmentary progression.',
    visualConcept: 'A shallow charcoal mural with one layered scene.',
    motionConcept: 'Slow pan and minimal zoom.',
    audioConcept: 'Sparse sound and intentional silence.',
    sceneCount: 4,
    sceneCountReason: 'The article has a sequence of distinct causal turns.',
    selectedFlags: selected,
    effectiveFlags: resolved.effectiveFlags,
    constraints: { noDialogueNarration: false, styleReferenceAvailable: false, guernicaLocationReference: false, articleImageAvailable: false, sceneCount: { minimum: 1, maximum: 12 }, ...constraints },
    reason: 'The article benefits from restrained visual continuity.',
    constraintsApplied: resolved.constraintsApplied,
  };
};

const response = (value) => ({ choices: [{ finish_reason: 'stop', message: { content: JSON.stringify(value) } }] });

describe('EU RESKI auto director', () => {
  test('defaults to off and accepts configuration and CLI activation', () => {
    expect(resolveAutoModeSetting()).toBe(false);
    expect(resolveAutoModeSetting(true)).toBe(true);
    expect(resolveAutoModeSetting(false, ['--auto-mode'])).toBe(true);
    expect(resolveAutoModeSetting(true, ['--no-auto-mode'])).toBe(false);
    expect(() => resolveAutoModeSetting(false, ['--auto-mode', '--no-auto-mode'])).toThrow(/Choose either/);
  });

  test('builds a structured request with the configured Story model and bounded article input', () => {
    const request = buildAutoDirectorRequest({
      article: { id: 'a1', title: 'Headline', text: 'x'.repeat(19000) },
      model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      constraints: { noDialogueNarration: true, styleReferenceAvailable: true },
    });
    expect(request.model).toBe('gpt-4.1-mini');
    expect(request.response_format.json_schema.schema).toEqual(autoDirectorSchema);
    expect(JSON.parse(request.messages[1].content).article.text).toHaveLength(18000);
    expect(JSON.parse(request.messages[1].content).fixedConstraints.noDialogueNarration).toBe(true);
    expect(request.max_completion_tokens).toBe(3000);
  });

  test('accepts a complete decision and rejects unknown or unsupported flags', () => {
    const value = {
      dramaturgicalConcept: 'A cycle.', visualConcept: 'Cubist planes.', motionConcept: 'Slow pan.',
      audioConcept: 'Silence.', sceneCount: 4, sceneCountReason: 'Four distinct actions make the turn legible.',
      flags: flags(), reason: 'A quiet treatment suits the story.',
    };
    expect(validateAutoDirectorResponse(response(value)).flags).toEqual(flags());
    expect(() => validateAutoDirectorResponse(response({ ...value, flags: { ...flags(), surprise: true } })))
      .toThrow(/unknown settings/);
    expect(() => validateAutoDirectorResponse(response({ ...value, flags: flags({ videoMaxZoom: 0.31 }) })))
      .toThrow(/videoMaxZoom/);
    expect(() => validateAutoDirectorResponse(response({ ...value, flags: flags({ videoVision: 'on', videoDirection: 'off' }) })))
      .toThrow(/without videoDirection/);
    expect(() => validateAutoDirectorResponse(response({ ...value, sceneCount: 8 }), { sceneCount: { minimum: 2, maximum: 6 } }))
      .toThrow(/sceneCount must be an integer/);
  });

  test('records selected and effective legacy behavior separately', () => {
    const selected = flags({ legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: true });
    const { effectiveFlags, constraintsApplied } = resolveEffectiveAutoFlags(selected, { styleReferenceAvailable: true });
    expect(selected.preserveCubistVideoStyle).toBe(true);
    expect(selected.sceneVisualControls).toBe('scene-specific');
    expect(effectiveFlags.preserveCubistVideoStyle).toBe(false);
    expect(effectiveFlags.sceneVisualControls).toBe('fixed');
    expect(constraintsApplied).toContain('Legacy Guernica director suppresses the additional Cubist Video Lock.');
    expect(resolveEffectiveAutoFlags(selected, { styleReferenceAvailable: true, guernicaLocationReference: true })
      .effectiveFlags.legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt).toBe(false);
  });

  test('keeps per-iteration decisions independent and fixes the executable video mode', () => {
    const first = flags({ videoMaxZoom: 0.2, articleImageReference: true });
    const second = flags({ videoMaxZoom: 0, articleImageReference: false, storyClou: 'on' });
    const effectiveFirst = resolveEffectiveAutoFlags(first).effectiveFlags;
    const effectiveSecond = resolveEffectiveAutoFlags(second).effectiveFlags;
    expect(effectiveFirst.videoMaxZoom).toBe(0.2);
    expect(effectiveSecond.videoMaxZoom).toBe(0);
    expect(effectiveFirst.articleImageReference).toBe(true);
    expect(effectiveSecond.articleImageReference).toBe(false);
    expect(effectiveFirst.videoMode).toBe('image_to_video');
    expect(effectiveSecond.videoMode).toBe('image_to_video');
    expect(first).not.toBe(second);
  });

  test('reuses only a matching saved decision and refuses missing or mismatched resume state', () => {
    const saved = decision(flags(), { constraints: {} });
    expect(resolveSavedAutoDecision(saved, {
      iteration: 1, articleId: 'article-1', model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, hasDependentWork: true, constraints: saved.constraints,
    })).toBe(saved);
    expect(() => resolveSavedAutoDecision(null, {
      iteration: 1, articleId: 'article-1', model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, hasDependentWork: true,
    })).toThrow(/refusing to choose new settings/);
    expect(resolveSavedAutoDecision(null, {
      iteration: 1, articleId: 'article-1', model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, hasDependentWork: false,
    })).toBeNull();
    expect(() => resolveSavedAutoDecision(saved, {
      iteration: 2, articleId: 'article-1', model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, hasDependentWork: true, constraints: saved.constraints,
    })).toThrow(/does not match/);
    expect(() => resolveSavedAutoDecision(saved, {
      iteration: 1, articleId: 'article-1', model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, hasDependentWork: true,
      constraints: { noDialogueNarration: true, styleReferenceAvailable: false, guernicaLocationReference: false, articleImageAvailable: false },
    })).toThrow(/fixed constraints changed/);
    expect(() => resolveSavedAutoDecision(saved, {
      iteration: 1, articleId: 'article-1', model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, hasDependentWork: true,
      constraints: { ...saved.constraints, sceneCount: { minimum: 2, maximum: 5 } },
    })).toThrow(/scene-count limits changed/);
    expect(() => resolveSavedAutoDecision(saved, {
      iteration: 1, articleId: 'article-1', model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, hasDependentWork: true,
      constraints: { ...saved.constraints, imageProvider: 'different-provider' },
    })).toThrow(/technical constraints changed/);
  });

  test('reuses historical V1 decisions without upgrading or reinterpreting them', () => {
    const selected = flags();
    const resolved = resolveEffectiveAutoFlags(selected, {});
    const saved = {
      autoMode: true,
      iteration: 1,
      articleId: 'article-1',
      model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: 'eu-reski-auto-director-v1',
      dramaturgicalConcept: 'The saved V1 dramaturgy.',
      visualConcept: 'The saved V1 image idea.',
      motionConcept: 'The saved V1 motion.',
      audioConcept: 'The saved V1 sound.',
      selectedFlags: selected,
      effectiveFlags: resolved.effectiveFlags,
      constraints: { noDialogueNarration: false, styleReferenceAvailable: false, guernicaLocationReference: false, articleImageAvailable: false },
      constraintsApplied: resolved.constraintsApplied,
      reason: 'Saved historical decision.',
    };
    expect(resolveSavedAutoDecision(saved, {
      iteration: 1, articleId: 'article-1', model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, hasDependentWork: true, constraints: saved.constraints,
    })).toBe(saved);
  });

  test('never requests a new paid Auto decision while resuming an existing Auto iteration', () => {
    expect(resolveAutoDecisionAction({ autoMode: true, savedDecisionExists: true, isResume: true, savedAutoMode: true }))
      .toBe('reuse');
    expect(() => resolveAutoDecisionAction({
      autoMode: true, savedDecisionExists: false, hasDependentWork: false, isResume: true, savedAutoMode: true,
    })).toThrow(/refusing to issue a new paid request/);
    expect(() => resolveAutoDecisionAction({
      autoMode: true, savedDecisionExists: false, hasDependentWork: true, isResume: false, savedAutoMode: true,
    })).toThrow(/dependent iteration work/);
  });
});

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/exhibition-runner-startup.test.js
import { access, mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { articleKey } from '../../eu-reski-news/scripts/news-eu-relevance.mjs';

const repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL('../..', import.meta.url));
const runnerPath = join(repoRoot, 'eu-reski-news/scripts/exhibition-runner.mjs');
const sceneDirectory = join(repoRoot, 'eu-reski-news/scenes');

async function waitForSettingsSnapshot(child, getOutput, timeoutMs = 10000) {
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  while (Date.now() - startedAt < timeoutMs) {
    const output = getOutput();
    if (output.includes('Settings snapshot:')) return output;
    if (child.exitCode !== null) throw new Error(`Exhibition runner exited before settings snapshot.\n${output}`);
    await new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, 25));
  }
  child.kill('SIGTERM');
  throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for runner startup.\n${getOutput()}`);
}

async function waitForParallelWorkers(child, getOutput, timeoutMs = 10000) {
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  while (Date.now() - startedAt < timeoutMs) {
    const output = getOutput();
    if ((output.match(/^Run folder: /gm) || []).length >= 2) return output;
    if (child.exitCode !== null) throw new Error(`Exhibition runner exited before both workers started.\n${output}`);
    await new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, 25));
  }
  child.kill('SIGTERM');
  throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for both scene workers.\n${getOutput()}`);
}

describe('Exhibition Runner startup', () => {
  test('starts independent workers and maps defaults and overrides through the real startup path', async () => {
    const temporaryRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-exhibition-startup-'));
    const newsDirectory = join(temporaryRoot, 'news');
    const outputDirectory = join(temporaryRoot, 'output');
    const semanticPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'semantic.json');
    const networkLogPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'unexpected-network.log');
    const networkBlockerPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'block-network.cjs');
    const article = {
      id: 'startup-story',
      title: 'A mock European story',
      text: 'Offline article fixture for testing exhibition startup and scene argument mapping.',
      summary: 'An offline startup fixture.',
      image: 'https://mock.invalid/article.png',
      url: 'https://mock.invalid/story',
      source_country: 'EU',
      publish_date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    };
    let child;
    let output = '';
    let errorOutput = '';
    try {
      await mkdir(newsDirectory, { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(join(newsDirectory, 'all-startup.json'), JSON.stringify({
        date: article.publish_date,
        fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        news: [article],
      }));
      await writeFile(join(newsDirectory, 'eu-relevance-startup.json'), JSON.stringify({
        sourceFile: 'all-startup.json',
        articleCount: 1,
        completedCount: 1,
        failedCount: 0,
        unresolvedCount: 0,
        entries: { [articleKey(article)]: { euRelevance: 0.99 } },
      }));
      await writeFile(semanticPath, JSON.stringify({
        provider: 'offline-test',
        capturedAt: '2026-10-28T12:00:00.000Z',
        words: [['EU', 'en']],
        entries: [{ id: 'offline-entry', seed: ['EU', 'en'], item: { title: 'Europe', summary: 'European cooperation.' } }],
      }));
      await writeFile(networkBlockerPath, [
        "const fs = require('node:fs');",
        'globalThis.fetch = async (input) => {',
        "  const url = typeof input === 'string' ? input : input.url;",
        '  fs.appendFileSync(process.env.TEST_NETWORK_LOG, `${url}\\n`);',
        '  throw new Error(`Network disabled in startup test: ${url}`);',
        '};',
      ].join('\n'));
      const oldRunDirectories = [join(outputDirectory, 'old-run-a'), join(outputDirectory, 'old-run-b')];
      await Promise.all(oldRunDirectories.map(async (directory, index) => {
        await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });
        await writeFile(join(directory, 'run.json'), JSON.stringify({ status: 'completed', iterations: [] }));
        await writeFile(join(directory, 'keep.txt'), `old-run-${index + 1}`);
      }));
      await mkdir(outputDirectory, { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(join(outputDirectory, 'exhibition-state.json'), JSON.stringify({
        scenePath: sceneDirectory,
        scenePaths: [sceneDirectory],
        sceneWorkers: {
          '01-run-001-exhibition': { status: 'completed', runDirectory: oldRunDirectories[0] },
          '02-run-005-prompt-path': { status: 'completed', runDirectory: oldRunDirectories[1] },
        },
      }));

      child = spawn(process.execPath, [
        runnerPath,
        '--new-run',
        `--scene=${sceneDirectory}`,
        '--stop-after-iterations=1',
        '--prompt-only',
        '--no-first-last-glue',
        '--video-max-zoom=0.2',
        `--stream-file=${semanticPath}`,
      ], {
        cwd: repoRoot,
        env: {
          ...process.env,
          NEWS_NEWS_DIR: newsDirectory,
          NEWS_OUTPUT_DIR: outputDirectory,
          NEWS_REFRESH_CHECK_MS: '100000',
          OPENAI_API_KEY: 'offline-test-key',
          TEST_NETWORK_LOG: networkLogPath,
          NODE_OPTIONS: `--require=${networkBlockerPath}`,
        },
        stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
      });
      child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => { output += chunk.toString(); });
      child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => { errorOutput += chunk.toString(); });

      await waitForSettingsSnapshot(child, () => `${output}\n${errorOutput}`);
      await waitForParallelWorkers(child, () => `${output}\n${errorOutput}`);

      const plan = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(outputDirectory, '.exhibition-iterator-plan.json'), 'utf8'));
      expect(plan).toMatchObject({
        scheduleEnabled: true,
        generationInterval: '60m',
        biasMode: 'on',
      });
      expect(plan.workers.map(({ name }) => name)).toEqual(['run-001-exhibition', 'run-005-prompt-path']);
      expect(plan.workers.map(({ ratePerHour }) => ratePerHour)).toEqual([10, 5]);
      expect(plan.workers.map(({ plan: workerPlan }) => workerPlan.intervalMs)).toEqual([360_000, 720_000]);
      const statePath = join(outputDirectory, 'exhibition-state.json');
      let savedState;
      const stateDeadline = Date.now() + 3000;
      while (Date.now() < stateDeadline) {
        savedState = JSON.parse(await readFile(statePath, 'utf8').catch(() => 'null'));
        if (Object.keys(savedState?.sceneWorkers || {}).length === 2) break;
        await new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, 20));
      }
      const workerRuns = await Promise.all(Object.values(savedState.sceneWorkers).map(async (worker) =>
        JSON.parse(await readFile(join(worker.runDirectory, 'run.json'), 'utf8'))));
      expect(Object.values(savedState.sceneWorkers).map(({ runDirectory }) => runDirectory))
        .not.toEqual(oldRunDirectories);
      expect(await Promise.all(oldRunDirectories.map((directory) => readFile(join(directory, 'keep.txt'), 'utf8'))))
        .toEqual(['old-run-1', 'old-run-2']);
      expect(workerRuns.map(({ sceneSchedule }) => sceneSchedule.intervalMs).sort((left, right) => left - right))
        .toEqual([360_000, 720_000]);
      expect(workerRuns.map(({ iteratorNames }) => iteratorNames)).toEqual([
        ['run-001-exhibition'],
        ['run-005-prompt-path'],
      ]);
      expect(output.match(/^Run folder: /gm)).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(output).toContain('Starting 2 independent scene workers in parallel');
      expect(output).toContain('--stop-after-iterations=1 applies per worker; maximum total is 2 iterations.');
      const [auto, manual] = plan.workers.map(({ plan: workerPlan }) => workerPlan.iterators[0].arguments);
      const has = (args, value) => expect(args).toContain(value);

      has(auto, '--auto-mode');
      has(auto, '--story-model=gpt-4.1-mini');
      has(auto, '--no-preserve-cubist-video-style');
      has(auto, '--scene-count-mode=taktmuster');
      has(auto, '--scene-length-takt=4');
      has(auto, '--video-audio-direction=on');
      has(auto, '--article-order=global');
      has(auto, '--iterations=1');
      has(manual, '--no-auto-mode');
      has(manual, '--story-model=gpt-4o-mini');
      has(manual, '--article-order=global');
      has(manual, '--iterations=1');
      has(manual, '--preserve-cubist-video-style');
      has(manual, '--no-style-reference');
      has(manual, '--use-guernica-figure-reference');
      has(manual, '--article-image-reference');
      for (const args of [auto, manual]) {
        has(args, '--video-max-zoom=0.2');
        has(args, '--prompt-only');
        has(args, `--stream-file=${semanticPath}`);
        expect(args.filter((argument) => argument.startsWith('--video-max-zoom='))).toEqual(['--video-max-zoom=0.2']);
      }
      expect(output).toContain('Settings source:');
      expect(errorOutput).not.toContain('defaultsFromExhibitionSettings is not defined');
      await expect(access(networkLogPath)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ENOENT' });
    } finally {
      if (child && child.exitCode === null) {
        child.kill('SIGTERM');
        let timeoutId;
        await Promise.race([
          new Promise((resolveClose) => child.once('close', resolveClose)),
          new Promise((resolveTimeout) => { timeoutId = setTimeout(resolveTimeout, 3000); }),
        ]);
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      }
      await rm(temporaryRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });
});

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/auto-mode-startup.test.js
import { access, mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { articleKey } from '../../eu-reski-news/scripts/news-eu-relevance.mjs';

const runnerPath = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../eu-reski-news/scripts/run-news-scene-image-iteration.mjs', import.meta.url));

describe('new Auto Mode iteration startup', () => {
  test('sends Auto Director request and persists it when iteration has no saved Auto files', async () => {
    const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-auto-startup-'));
    const newsDirectory = join(root, 'news');
    const outputDirectory = join(root, 'output');
    const iterationDirectory = join(outputDirectory, 'iteration-001');
    const semanticPath = join(root, 'semantic.json');
    const fetchMockPath = join(root, 'mock-fetch.cjs');
    const callsPath = join(root, 'api-calls.json');
    const imageUrl = 'https://mock.invalid/story-image.png';
    const article = {
      id: 'story-1',
      title: 'EU policy test story',
      text: 'A new policy decision changes how European institutions coordinate shared work.',
      summary: 'A new European policy decision.',
      image: imageUrl,
      url: 'https://mock.invalid/story',
      source_country: 'EU',
      publish_date: '2026-10-28',
    };
    try {
      await mkdir(newsDirectory, { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(join(newsDirectory, 'all-test.json'), JSON.stringify({ date: '2026-10-28', news: [article] }));
      await writeFile(join(newsDirectory, 'eu-relevance-test.json'), JSON.stringify({
        sourceFile: 'all-test.json',
        entries: { [articleKey(article)]: { euRelevance: 0.95 } },
      }));
      await writeFile(semanticPath, JSON.stringify({
        provider: 'offline-test',
        capturedAt: '2026-10-28T12:00:00.000Z',
        words: [['EU', 'en']],
        entries: [{ id: 'mock-entry', seed: ['EU', 'en'], item: { title: 'Europe', summary: 'European cooperation.' } }],
      }));
      await writeFile(fetchMockPath, [
        "const fs = require('node:fs');",
        'let openAiCalls = [];',
        'globalThis.fetch = async (input, init = {}) => {',
        "  const url = typeof input === 'string' ? input : input.url;",
        '  if (url === process.env.TEST_IMAGE_URL) {',
        "    return new Response(Buffer.from('offline image bytes'), { headers: { 'content-type': 'image/png' } });",
        '  }',
        "  if (url === 'https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions') {",
        '    const body = JSON.parse(init.body);',
        '    openAiCalls.push({ url, body });',
        '    fs.writeFileSync(process.env.TEST_API_CALLS_PATH, JSON.stringify(openAiCalls, null, 2));',
        '    if (openAiCalls.length === 1) {',
        '      return new Response(JSON.stringify({',
        '        choices: [{ finish_reason: "stop", message: { content: JSON.stringify({',
        '          dramaturgicalConcept: "Follow the decision through two clear turns.",',
        '          visualConcept: "Use a restrained, symbolic composition.",',
        '          motionConcept: "Keep movement slow and deliberate.",',
        '          audioConcept: "Use quiet ambient sound and pauses.",',
        '          sceneCount: 2,',
        '          sceneCountReason: "Two scenes express the decision and its immediate consequence.",',
        '          flags: {',
        '            storyClou: "off", legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: false,',
        '            preserveCubistVideoStyle: false, sceneVisualControls: "scene-specific",',
        '            articleImageReference: false, landscapePrompt: false,',
        '            videoDirection: "off", videoVision: "off", videoAudioDirection: "off", videoMaxZoom: 0.08,',
        '          },',
        '          reason: "A compact form fits this test article.",',
        '        }) } }],',
        '        usage: { prompt_tokens: 20, completion_tokens: 10, total_tokens: 30 },',
        '      }), { headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });',
        '    }',
        '    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: { message: "Stop after verifying Auto Director startup." } }), {',
        '      status: 400, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },',
        '    });',
        '  }',
        '  throw new Error(`Unexpected network request in offline test: ${url}`);',
        '};',
      ].join('\n'));
      let iterationExistedBeforeStart = true;
      try {
        await access(iterationDirectory);
      } catch {
        iterationExistedBeforeStart = false;
      }
      expect(iterationExistedBeforeStart).toBe(false);
      const result = spawnSync(process.execPath, [
        '--require', fetchMockPath,
        runnerPath,
        '--auto-mode', '--prompt-only', '--iterations=1', '--article-id=story-1',
        `--news-dir=${newsDirectory}`, `--output-dir=${outputDirectory}`, `--stream-file=${semanticPath}`,
        '--no-style-reference', '--no-guernica-figure-reference',
      ], {
        encoding: 'utf8',
        timeout: 20000,
        env: {
          ...process.env,
          OPENAI_API_KEY: 'offline-test-key',
          TEST_IMAGE_URL: imageUrl,
          TEST_API_CALLS_PATH: callsPath,
        },
      });

      expect(result.status).not.toBe(0);
      expect(result.stderr).toContain('gpt: HTTP 400');
      const calls = JSON.parse(await readFile(callsPath, 'utf8'));
      expect(calls).toHaveLength(2);
      const savedRequest = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(iterationDirectory, 'auto-director-request.json'), 'utf8'));
      expect(calls[0].body).toEqual(savedRequest);
      expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(iterationDirectory, 'auto-director-response.json'), 'utf8')).choices[0].message.content)
        .toContain('sceneCountReason');
      expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(iterationDirectory, 'auto-decisions.json'), 'utf8')).sceneCount).toBe(2);
    } finally {
      await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });
});

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/exhibition-readiness.test.js
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { autoDirectorPromptVersion } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/auto-director.mjs';
import { isReadyForExhibition } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/exhibition-readiness.mjs';

const scene = (index) => ({ index });

describe('exhibition readiness', () => {
  test('allows new one-scene Auto iterations with zero glue bridges', () => {
    const autoSingleScene = {
      status: 'Abgeschlossen',
      autoMode: true,
      autoDecision: { promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion },
      sceneCount: 1,
      plan: { scenes: [scene(1)] },
    };
    expect(isReadyForExhibition(autoSingleScene, [true])).toBe(true);
    expect(isReadyForExhibition(autoSingleScene, [false])).toBe(false);
  });

  test('keeps manual and historical singleton behavior, while allowing later iterations after Auto singleton', () => {
    const manualSingleScene = { status: 'Abgeschlossen', plan: { scenes: [scene(1)] } };
    const historicalAuto = {
      status: 'Abgeschlossen',
      autoMode: true,
      autoDecision: { promptVersion: 'eu-reski-auto-director-v1' },
      sceneCount: 1,
      plan: { scenes: [scene(1)] },
    };
    const manualTwoScenes = { status: 'Abgeschlossen', plan: { scenes: [scene(1), scene(2)] } };
    expect(isReadyForExhibition(manualSingleScene, [true])).toBe(false);
    expect(isReadyForExhibition(historicalAuto, [true])).toBe(false);
    expect([isReadyForExhibition({
      status: 'Abgeschlossen',
      autoMode: true,
      autoDecision: { promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion },
      sceneCount: 1,
      plan: { scenes: [scene(1)] },
    }, [true]), isReadyForExhibition(manualTwoScenes, [true, true])]).toEqual([true, true]);
  });
});

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/runner-arguments.test.js
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { mergeRunnerArguments } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/runner-arguments.mjs';

const paths = { newsDirectory: '/news', outputDirectory: '/output' };

describe('exhibition CLI argument precedence', () => {
  test('explicit disable flags override scene/default settings', () => {
    const merged = mergeRunnerArguments([
      '--use-style-reference', '--use-guernica-figure-reference',
      '--preserve-cubist-video-style', '--videos-after-each-iteration',
    ], [
      '--no-style-reference', '--no-guernica-figure-reference',
      '--no-preserve-cubist-video-style', '--no-videos-after-each-iteration',
      '--iterator-plan=/saved/plan.json',
    ], paths);

    for (const flag of ['--use-style-reference', '--use-guernica-figure-reference', '--preserve-cubist-video-style', '--videos-after-each-iteration']) {
      expect(merged).not.toContain(flag);
    }
    expect(merged).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      '--no-style-reference', '--no-guernica-figure-reference',
      '--no-preserve-cubist-video-style', '--no-videos-after-each-iteration',
      '--iterator-plan=/saved/plan.json',
    ]));
  });

  test('explicit enable flags override disabled defaults and finite iteration count follows exhibition limit', () => {
    const merged = mergeRunnerArguments([
      '--no-style-reference', '--no-guernica-figure-reference',
      '--no-preserve-cubist-video-style', '--no-videos-after-each-iteration',
      '--iterations=-1',
    ], [
      '--use-style-reference', '--use-guernica-figure-reference',
      '--preserve-cubist-video-style', '--videos-after-each-iteration',
      '--iterations=3',
    ], { ...paths, stopAfterIterations: 3 });

    for (const flag of ['--no-style-reference', '--no-guernica-figure-reference', '--no-preserve-cubist-video-style', '--no-videos-after-each-iteration', '--iterations=-1']) {
      expect(merged).not.toContain(flag);
    }
    expect(merged).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([
      '--use-style-reference', '--use-guernica-figure-reference',
      '--preserve-cubist-video-style', '--videos-after-each-iteration', '--iterations=3',
    ]));
    expect(() => mergeRunnerArguments([], ['--iterations=4'], { ...paths, stopAfterIterations: 3 }))
      .toThrow(/stop-after-iterations/);
  });
});

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/historical-auto-v1-resume.test.js
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import {
  autoDirectorPromptVersion,
  resolveAutoDecisionAction,
  resolveSavedAutoDecision,
} from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/auto-director.mjs';

const fixturePath = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), 'fixtures', 'historical-auto-v1-run-001.json');
const fixture = JSON.parse(await readFile(fixturePath, 'utf8'));

describe('historical Auto Director V1 resume', () => {
  test('reuses actual run-001 decision, count, technical settings, provider tasks, and concept-free requests', () => {
    const saved = structuredClone(fixture.autoDecision);
    const record = structuredClone(fixture.record);
    const decision = resolveSavedAutoDecision(saved, {
      iteration: saved.iteration,
      articleId: saved.articleId,
      model: saved.model,
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion,
      hasDependentWork: true,
      constraints: saved.constraints,
    });

    expect(resolveAutoDecisionAction({
      autoMode: true,
      savedDecisionExists: true,
      hasDependentWork: true,
      isResume: true,
      savedAutoMode: true,
    })).toBe('reuse');
    expect(decision.promptVersion).toBe('eu-reski-auto-director-v1');
    expect(record.sceneCount).toBe(5);
    expect(record.sceneDurations).toEqual([8, 5, 7, 5, 8]);
    expect(record.sceneCountSelection).toMatchObject({
      count: 5,
      settings: { mode: 'taktmuster', numerator: 4, denominator: 4, bias: 1 },
    });
    expect(record.videoTaskIds).toHaveLength(5);
    expect(record.videoTaskIds).toContain('be2b51cc-c457-4f46-b481-329ba4dc94a0');
    expect(record.generationSettings.videoModel).toBe('prunaai:p-video@2-pro');
    expect(record.generationSettings.videoResolution).toBe('480p');
    expect(record.generationSettings.videoSettingsMode).toBe('cost');
    expect(record.generationSettings.imageProvider).toBe('hf-flux-klein');
    expect(record.featureSettings.videoVision).toBe('on');
    expect(record.featureSettings.videoAudioDirection).toBe('on');
    expect(Object.values(fixture.promptChecks).every(({ hasAutoConceptHandoff }) => !hasAutoConceptHandoff)).toBe(true);
    expect(fixture.requestHashes).toEqual({
      'gpt-request.json': '898241721e8e0e6eb3fb3c3548cbf8dd8e4b03a3f21995fb180a146f60192c9b',
      'timing-request.json': '0ef8bde7b551361324931dcf07446af02face4ae2aaedb1ddc335bf52d238057',
      'video-direction-request.json': '27dafb97235acd46cb27075072b3f6fc473637fdb54212b49ad26674f464b2eb',
      'audio-direction-request.json': '15cff4abef913f7b47b699d1369e9fce6256e1a261a13b9f1ddcd3a36b7ea300',
      'film-plan.json': 'ae3901dcd7893a95e6976466fb68123498286487760305fd26cf256719c04d70',
    });
    expect(saved).toEqual(fixture.autoDecision);
  });
});

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/auto-scene-count.test.js
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import {
  createAutoSceneCountSelection,
  createSceneCountStream,
  isNewAutoSingleScene,
  replaySavedSceneCount,
  resolveAutoSceneCountLimits,
  resolveSceneCountSettings,
} from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/scene-count.mjs';
import { autoDirectorPromptVersion, validateAutoDirectorResponse } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/auto-director.mjs';
import { buildVideoDirectionRequest } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/video-director.mjs';

class MockTaktmuster {
  static lastInstance;
  constructor() {
    this.calls = 0;
    MockTaktmuster.lastInstance = this;
  }
  calls = 0;
  setTakt() {}
  setType() {}
  getNext() {
    this.calls += 1;
    return { patternValue: this.calls + 3 };
  }
}

const response = (sceneCount, sceneCountReason) => ({ choices: [{ finish_reason: 'stop', message: { content: JSON.stringify({
  dramaturgicalConcept: 'A causal arc.', visualConcept: 'An evolving mural.', motionConcept: 'A restrained crossing.', audioConcept: 'Wind and silence.',
  sceneCount, sceneCountReason,
  flags: {
    storyClou: 'off', legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: false, preserveCubistVideoStyle: false,
    sceneVisualControls: 'fixed', articleImageReference: false, landscapePrompt: false,
    videoDirection: 'off', videoVision: 'off', videoAudioDirection: 'off', videoMaxZoom: 0,
  },
  reason: 'The choices fit the supplied article.',
}) } }] });

describe('Auto Director scene count selection', () => {
  test('mocked articles can select different counts, both bounded by configuration', () => {
    const limits = resolveAutoSceneCountLimits({ minimum: 2, maximum: 6 });
    const simpleArticle = validateAutoDirectorResponse(response(2, 'The short report supports a compact two-beat arc.'), { sceneCount: limits });
    const complexArticle = validateAutoDirectorResponse(response(5, 'The report contains several distinct turns that need five beats.'), { sceneCount: limits });

    expect(simpleArticle.sceneCount).toBe(2);
    expect(complexArticle.sceneCount).toBe(5);
    expect(() => validateAutoDirectorResponse(response(7, 'Too many.'), { sceneCount: limits }))
      .toThrow(/sceneCount must be an integer/);
  });

  test('manual fixed and Taktmuster modes keep their existing count selection', () => {
    const fixed = createSceneCountStream(resolveSceneCountSettings({ 'scene-count-mode': 'fixed', scenes: 4 }));
    expect(fixed.next().count).toBe(4);
    expect(fixed.next().count).toBe(4);

    const stream = createSceneCountStream(resolveSceneCountSettings({
      'scene-count-mode': 'taktmuster', 'scene-count-takt': 2, 'scene-count-numerator': 3,
      'scene-count-denominator': 4, 'scene-count-type': 'balanced', 'scene-count-bias': 2,
      'min-scenes': 1, 'max-scenes': 12,
    }), MockTaktmuster);
    expect(stream.next().count).toBe(6);
    expect(stream.next().count).toBe(7);
    expect(MockTaktmuster.lastInstance.calls).toBe(2);
  });

  test('new Auto selections are saved with limits and do not consume a Taktmuster step on replay', () => {
    const bounds = { minimum: 2, maximum: 6 };
    const selection = createAutoSceneCountSelection({
      promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, sceneCount: 4,
      sceneCountReason: 'Four causal changes form the clearest arc.',
    }, 0, bounds);
    const stream = createSceneCountStream(resolveSceneCountSettings({
      'scene-count-mode': 'taktmuster', 'scene-count-takt': 2, 'scene-count-numerator': 3,
      'scene-count-denominator': 4, 'scene-count-type': 'balanced', 'scene-count-bias': 2,
      'min-scenes': 1, 'max-scenes': 12,
    }), MockTaktmuster);
    const saved = { sceneCount: 4, autoDecision: { promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, sceneCount: 4 } };
    const replayed = replaySavedSceneCount(saved, stream);

    expect(selection).toMatchObject({ count: 4, reason: 'Four causal changes form the clearest arc.', settings: bounds });
    expect(replayed.count).toBe(4);
    expect(replayed.replayed).toBe(false);
    expect(MockTaktmuster.lastInstance.calls).toBe(0);
  });

  test('historical Auto V1 replay still consumes its saved rhythm step and retains its count', () => {
    const stream = createSceneCountStream(resolveSceneCountSettings({
      'scene-count-mode': 'taktmuster', 'scene-count-takt': 2, 'scene-count-numerator': 3,
      'scene-count-denominator': 4, 'scene-count-type': 'balanced', 'scene-count-bias': 2,
      'min-scenes': 1, 'max-scenes': 12,
    }), MockTaktmuster);
    const selection = replaySavedSceneCount({ sceneCount: 6, autoDecision: { promptVersion: 'eu-reski-auto-director-v1' } }, stream);

    expect(selection.count).toBe(6);
    expect(selection.replayed).toBe(true);
    expect(MockTaktmuster.lastInstance.calls).toBe(1);
  });

  test('interrupted fixed-count iterations retain their existing count validation', () => {
    const stream = createSceneCountStream(resolveSceneCountSettings({ 'scene-count-mode': 'fixed', scenes: 4 }));
    expect(() => replaySavedSceneCount({ sceneCount: 5 }, stream, { validateFixed: true }))
      .toThrow(/Saved scene-count rhythm differs/);
  });

  test('one-scene video direction requests require no adjacent transitions', () => {
    const record = {
      plan: { storyPremise: 'A single held image.', protagonist: { name: 'Mira' }, scenes: [{
        index: 1, title: 'Stillness', storyBeat: 'A single reveal.', protagonistAction: 'Mira holds a lantern.',
        durationSeconds: 5, videoPrompt: 'Mira holds the lantern steady and its small painted flame settles into stillness.',
      }] },
      results: [{ path: 'scene-1.png', sha256: 'a'.repeat(64) }],
    };
    const request = buildVideoDirectionRequest({ record, model: 'gpt-4.1-mini', videoMode: 'image_to_video' });
    const transitions = request.response_format.json_schema.schema.properties.sceneTransitions;

    expect(transitions.minItems).toBe(0);
    expect(transitions.maxItems).toBe(0);
    expect(request.response_format.json_schema.schema.properties.scenes.minItems).toBe(1);
    expect(isNewAutoSingleScene({
      autoMode: true,
      sceneCount: 1,
      autoDecision: { promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion },
      plan: { scenes: [{ index: 1 }] },
    })).toBe(true);
    expect(isNewAutoSingleScene({
      sceneCount: 1,
      autoDecision: { promptVersion: 'eu-reski-auto-director-v1' },
      plan: { scenes: [{ index: 1 }] },
    })).toBe(false);
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, jest, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { appendFile, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { WordStream } from 'semantic-stream';

import {
  createSemanticSource,
  WIKIPEDIA_REQUEST_POLICY,
  WIKIPEDIA_USER_AGENT,
} from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/semantic-source.mjs';

let temporaryDirectory;

afterEach(async () => {
  if (temporaryDirectory) {
    await rm(temporaryDirectory, { recursive: true, force: true });
    temporaryDirectory = undefined;
  }
});

describe('EU-RESKI semantic source', () => {
  test('stops after bounded 429 retries instead of falling back to another search', async () => {
    const throttled = Object.assign(new Error('Too Many Requests'), { status: 429 });
    const page = jest.fn(() => Promise.reject(throttled));
    const search = jest.fn();
    const stream = new WordStream('EU', 'de', {
      wikiFactory: () => ({ page, search }),
      scheduleWikipediaRequest: (operation) => operation(),
      requestPolicy: {
        maxAttempts: 3,
        baseDelayMs: 0,
        maxDelayMs: 0,
        jitterRatio: 0,
        wait: async () => {},
        onRetry: () => {},
      },
    });

    await expect(stream.start()).rejects.toBe(throttled);
    expect(page).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    expect(search).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('passes conservative Wikipedia policy and identifiable User-Agent to streams', async () => {
    temporaryDirectory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'semantic-source-test-'));
    const optionsPath = join(temporaryDirectory, 'captured-options.json');
    const modulePath = join(temporaryDirectory, 'mock-semantic-stream.mjs');

    await writeFile(modulePath, [
      "import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';",
      `export async function initStreams(words, options) { await writeFile(${JSON.stringify(optionsPath)}, JSON.stringify(options)); return [{ getNext: async () => ({ title: 'Test article' }) }]; }`,
    ].join('\n'));

    const source = await createSemanticSource({ words: 'EU', 'stream-module': modulePath });
    const capturedOptions = JSON.parse(await readFile(optionsPath, 'utf8'));
    const semantic = await source.next();

    expect(capturedOptions.wordStreamOptions).toEqual({
      userAgent: WIKIPEDIA_USER_AGENT,
      requestPolicy: WIKIPEDIA_REQUEST_POLICY,
    });
    expect(semantic.entries[0].item.title).toBe('Test article');
    expect(WIKIPEDIA_REQUEST_POLICY).toEqual({
      minIntervalMs: 3000,
      maxAttempts: 3,
      baseDelayMs: 60000,
      maxDelayMs: 300000,
      jitterRatio: 0.2,
    });
  });

  test('restores the same pending semantic snapshot and continues the saved WordStream state', async () => {
    temporaryDirectory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'semantic-source-checkpoint-test-'));
    const modulePath = join(temporaryDirectory, 'mock-semantic-stream.mjs');
    const initializationLog = join(temporaryDirectory, 'initializations.log');
    const checkpointPath = join(temporaryDirectory, 'semantic-stream-state.json');
    await writeFile(modulePath, [
      "import { appendFile } from 'node:fs/promises';",
      `const initializationLog = ${JSON.stringify(initializationLog)};`,
      'export class WordStream {',
      '  constructor(word, lang) { this.word = word; this.lang = lang; this.circularLinks = { links: {}, usedLinks: {} }; }',
      '  async getNext() {',
      '    const index = Object.keys(this.circularLinks.usedLinks).length + 1;',
      '    const title = `${this.word} fragment ${index}`;',
      '    this.circularLinks.links[title] = { title };',
      '    this.circularLinks.usedLinks[title] = { cnt: 1 };',
      '    return { title, context: `context ${index}` };',
      '  }',
      '}',
      'export async function initStreams(words) {',
      '  await appendFile(initializationLog, "initialized\\n");',
      '  return words.map(([word, lang]) => new WordStream(word, lang));',
      '}',
    ].join('\n'));

    const options = { words: 'EU', 'stream-module': modulePath };
    const firstSource = await createSemanticSource(options, { checkpointPath });
    const interruptedSnapshot = await firstSource.next();
    const resumedSource = await createSemanticSource(options, { checkpointPath });

    expect(resumedSource.resumedFromCheckpoint).toBe(true);
    expect(await resumedSource.next()).toEqual(interruptedSnapshot);
    await resumedSource.acknowledge(interruptedSnapshot);
    const nextSnapshot = await resumedSource.next();
    expect(nextSnapshot.entries[0].item.title).toBe('EU fragment 2');
    expect((await readFile(initializationLog, 'utf8')).trim().split('\n')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  test('restores sequence cursor after an acknowledged snapshot', async () => {
    temporaryDirectory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'semantic-sequence-checkpoint-test-'));
    const sequencePath = join(temporaryDirectory, 'sequence.json');
    const checkpointPath = join(temporaryDirectory, 'semantic-stream-state.json');
    const snapshots = [1, 2].map((index) => ({
      provider: 'fixture',
      capturedAt: `2026-10-28T00:00:0${index}.000Z`,
      words: [['EU', 'de']],
      entries: [{ id: `stream-${index}`, seed: ['EU', 'de'], item: { title: `Fragment ${index}` } }],
    }));
    await writeFile(sequencePath, JSON.stringify(snapshots));

    const options = { 'stream-sequence-file': sequencePath };
    const firstSource = await createSemanticSource(options, { checkpointPath });
    const firstSnapshot = await firstSource.next();
    await firstSource.acknowledge(firstSnapshot);
    const resumedSource = await createSemanticSource(options, { checkpointPath });

    expect(await resumedSource.next()).toEqual(snapshots[1]);
  });
});

import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { autoDirectorPromptVersion, buildAutoDirectorRequest, resolveAutoDecisionAction } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/auto-director.mjs';
import {
  addUsageIdempotently,
  buildAutoDirectorCorrectionRequest,
  recoverAutoDirectorDecision,
  recoverSavedAutoDirectorDecision,
} from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/auto-director-recovery.mjs';

const constraints = {
  noDialogueNarration: false,
  styleReferenceAvailable: true,
  guernicaLocationReference: false,
  articleImageAvailable: true,
  fixedVisualStyleOptions: { symbolic: true, roomDeepness: false, layering: 3 },
  sceneCount: { minimum: 2, maximum: 8 },
};
const article = { id: 'article-7', title: 'A saved article', text: 'Saved story input.' };
const request = buildAutoDirectorRequest({ article, model: 'gpt-4.1-mini', constraints });
const flags = {
  storyClou: 'on',
  legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: false,
  preserveCubistVideoStyle: true,
  sceneVisualControls: 'scene-specific',
  articleImageReference: true,
  landscapePrompt: false,
  videoDirection: 'on',
  videoVision: 'on',
  videoAudioDirection: 'on',
  videoMaxZoom: 0.12,
};
const response = {
  choices: [{
    finish_reason: 'stop',
    message: { content: JSON.stringify({
      dramaturgicalConcept: 'Open with a quiet rupture and return to its cause.',
      visualConcept: 'Charcoal planes gather around one changing focal shape.',
      motionConcept: 'Slow lateral drift; no new camera grammar.',
      audioConcept: 'Low mechanical pulse with deliberate silence.',
      sceneCount: 5,
      sceneCountReason: 'Five causal turns keep the article legible without flattening its conflict.',
      flags,
      reason: 'A restrained visual progression supports the article.',
    }) },
  }],
  usage: { total_tokens: 123 },
};

describe('Auto Director response crash recovery', () => {
  test('integrates saved response files into decision persistence without provider access', async () => {
    const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'auto-director-recovery-'));
    const record = { usage: { prompt_tokens: 10, completion_tokens: 2, total_tokens: 12 } };
    let persistCalls = 0;
    try {
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'auto-director-request.json'), JSON.stringify(request));
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'auto-director-response.json'), JSON.stringify(response));
      const recovered = await recoverSavedAutoDirectorDecision({
        directory,
        expectedRequest: request,
        articleId: article.id,
        iteration: 3,
        model: request.model,
        constraints,
        record,
        applyDecision: (target, decision) => { target.recovered = decision.sceneCount; },
        persist: async () => { persistCalls += 1; },
      });

      expect(recovered.sceneCount).toBe(5);
      expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(directory, 'auto-decisions.json'), 'utf8'))).toEqual(recovered);
      expect(record.recovered).toBe(5);
      expect(record.usage).toMatchObject({ prompt_tokens: 10, completion_tokens: 2, total_tokens: 135 });
      expect(record.autoDirectorUsage).toEqual(response.usage);
      expect(persistCalls).toBe(1);

      const resumed = await recoverSavedAutoDirectorDecision({
        directory,
        expectedRequest: request,
        articleId: article.id,
        iteration: 3,
        model: request.model,
        constraints,
        record,
        applyDecision: () => { throw new Error('saved decision must be reused'); },
        persist: async () => { persistCalls += 1; },
      });
      expect(resumed).toBeNull();
      expect(persistCalls).toBe(1);
    } finally {
      await rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  test('saved response without original request fails before persistence', async () => {
    const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'auto-director-recovery-'));
    try {
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'auto-director-response.json'), JSON.stringify(response));
      let persistCalls = 0;
      await expect(recoverSavedAutoDirectorDecision({
        directory,
        expectedRequest: request,
        articleId: article.id,
        iteration: 3,
        model: request.model,
        constraints,
        record: {},
        applyDecision: () => {},
        persist: async () => { persistCalls += 1; },
      })).rejects.toThrow(/no original request/);
      expect(persistCalls).toBe(0);
    } finally {
      await rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  test('integrates saved correction request/response files after interruption', async () => {
    const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'auto-director-revision-recovery-'));
    const invalidResponse = structuredClone(response);
    invalidResponse.choices[0].message.content = '{"flags":{}}';
    let validationError;
    try {
      recoverAutoDirectorDecision({
        savedRequest: request,
        expectedRequest: request,
        response: invalidResponse,
        articleId: article.id,
        iteration: 3,
        model: request.model,
        constraints,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      validationError = error.message;
    }
    const revisionRequest = buildAutoDirectorCorrectionRequest(request, invalidResponse, validationError);
    const correctionResponse = { ...structuredClone(response), usage: { total_tokens: 77 } };
    let persistCalls = 0;
    const record = {};
    try {
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'auto-director-request.json'), JSON.stringify(request));
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'auto-director-response.json'), JSON.stringify(invalidResponse));
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'auto-director-revision-request.json'), JSON.stringify(revisionRequest));
      await writeFile(join(directory, 'auto-director-revision-response.json'), JSON.stringify(correctionResponse));
      const recovered = await recoverSavedAutoDirectorDecision({
        directory,
        expectedRequest: request,
        articleId: article.id,
        iteration: 3,
        model: request.model,
        constraints,
        record,
        applyDecision: (target, decision) => { target.sceneCount = decision.sceneCount; },
        persist: async () => { persistCalls += 1; },
      });
      expect(recovered.sceneCount).toBe(5);
      expect(record.autoDirectorUsage).toEqual({ total_tokens: 77 });
      expect(record.sceneCount).toBe(5);
      expect(persistCalls).toBe(1);
      expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(directory, 'auto-decisions.json'), 'utf8')).usage).toEqual({ total_tokens: 77 });
    } finally {
      await rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  test('rebuilds a decision from saved original response without another provider call', () => {
    const recovered = recoverAutoDirectorDecision({
      savedRequest: structuredClone(request),
      expectedRequest: request,
      response,
      articleId: article.id,
      iteration: 3,
      model: request.model,
      constraints,
    });

    expect(recovered.promptVersion).toBe(autoDirectorPromptVersion);
    expect(recovered.sceneCount).toBe(5);
    expect(recovered.audioConcept).toContain('silence');
    expect(recovered.recoveredFromSavedResponse).toBe(true);
    expect(recovered.conceptSnapshotHash).toMatch(/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/);
    expect(recovered.usage).toEqual(response.usage);
  });

  test('uses saved valid correction response after a crash before decision persistence', () => {
    const invalidResponse = structuredClone(response);
    invalidResponse.choices[0].message.content = '{"flags":{}}';
    let validationError;
    try {
      recoverAutoDirectorDecision({
        savedRequest: request,
        expectedRequest: request,
        response: invalidResponse,
        articleId: article.id,
        iteration: 3,
        model: request.model,
        constraints,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      validationError = error.message;
    }
    expect(validationError).toBeTruthy();

    const revisionRequest = buildAutoDirectorCorrectionRequest(request, invalidResponse, validationError);
    const correctionResponse = { ...structuredClone(response), usage: { total_tokens: 77 } };
    const recovered = recoverAutoDirectorDecision({
      savedRequest: request,
      expectedRequest: request,
      response: invalidResponse,
      revisionRequest,
      revisionResponse: correctionResponse,
      articleId: article.id,
      iteration: 3,
      model: request.model,
      constraints,
    });

    expect(recovered.sceneCount).toBe(5);
    expect(recovered.usage).toEqual({ total_tokens: 77 });
    expect(recovered.recoveredFromSavedResponse).toBe(true);
  });

  test('fails closed when saved correction response is invalid', () => {
    const invalidResponse = structuredClone(response);
    invalidResponse.choices[0].message.content = '{"flags":{}}';
    let validationError;
    try {
      recoverAutoDirectorDecision({
        savedRequest: request,
        expectedRequest: request,
        response: invalidResponse,
        articleId: article.id,
        iteration: 3,
        model: request.model,
        constraints,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      validationError = error.message;
    }
    const revisionRequest = buildAutoDirectorCorrectionRequest(request, invalidResponse, validationError);
    const invalidCorrection = structuredClone(invalidResponse);
    expect(() => recoverAutoDirectorDecision({
      savedRequest: request,
      expectedRequest: request,
      response: invalidResponse,
      revisionRequest,
      revisionResponse: invalidCorrection,
      articleId: article.id,
      iteration: 3,
      model: request.model,
      constraints,
    })).toThrow();
  });

  test('does not retry when correction request exists but its response is missing', () => {
    const invalidResponse = structuredClone(response);
    invalidResponse.choices[0].message.content = '{"flags":{}}';
    expect(() => recoverAutoDirectorDecision({
      savedRequest: request,
      expectedRequest: request,
      response: invalidResponse,
      revisionRequest: request,
      articleId: article.id,
      iteration: 3,
      model: request.model,
      constraints,
    })).toThrow(/no saved response; refusing to issue another paid request/);
  });

  test('fails closed when saved answer has no original request', () => {
    expect(() => recoverAutoDirectorDecision({

      expectedRequest: request,
      response,
      articleId: article.id,
      iteration: 3,
      model: request.model,
      constraints,
    })).toThrow(/request differs/);
  });

  test('adds token usage only once when decision recovery is repeated', () => {
    const record = { usage: { prompt_tokens: 9, completion_tokens: 4, total_tokens: 13 } };
    const recoveredUsage = { prompt_tokens: 20, completion_tokens: 6, total_tokens: 26 };
    addUsageIdempotently(record, 'autoDirectorUsage', recoveredUsage);
    const firstTotals = structuredClone(record.usage);
    addUsageIdempotently(record, 'autoDirectorUsage', recoveredUsage);
    expect(record.autoDirectorUsage).toEqual(recoveredUsage);
    expect(record.usage).toEqual(firstTotals);
    expect(record.usage).toEqual({ prompt_tokens: 29, completion_tokens: 10, total_tokens: 39 });
  });

  test('rejects a response when original saved request differs from resume inputs', () => {
    expect(() => recoverAutoDirectorDecision({
      savedRequest: { ...request, model: 'changed-model' },
      expectedRequest: request,
      response,
      articleId: article.id,
      iteration: 3,
      model: request.model,
      constraints,
    })).toThrow(/request differs/);
  });

  test('unknown provider outcome stays fail-closed while pending marker can be preserved', () => {
    expect(() => resolveAutoDecisionAction({
      autoMode: true,
      savedDecisionExists: false,
      hasDependentWork: true,
      isResume: true,
      savedAutoMode: true,
    })).toThrow(/refusing to issue a new paid request/);
  });
});

import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { buildAudioDirectionRequest, buildAudioPrompt } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/audio-director.mjs';
import { buildImageTask, buildPlanRequest } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/planner.mjs';
import { buildTimingRequest } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/scene-timing.mjs';
import { buildGrokVideoPrompt, buildVideoDirectionRequest } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/video-director.mjs';

const sha256 = (value) => createHash('sha256')
  .update(typeof value === 'string' ? value : JSON.stringify(value))
  .digest('hex');

const article = {
  id: 'golden-article',
  title: 'A bridge opens between two towns',
  publish_date: '2026-01-02',
  url: 'https://example.test/a',
  source_country: 'Belgium',
  language: 'en',
  summary: 'A new bridge connects two towns.',
  text: 'Residents cross a newly opened bridge.',
};
const semantic = { entries: [] };
const catalog = [];
const images = [];
const visualStyleOptions = { symbolic: true, roomDeepness: false, layering: 2 };
const plan = {
  storyPremise: 'A bridge joins separated routines.',
  protagonist: { name: 'Mira' },
  scenes: [1, 2].map((index) => ({
    index,
    title: `Scene ${index}`,
    storyBeat: `Beat ${index}`,
    protagonistAction: `Mira crosses section ${index}.`,
    dramaticFunction: index === 1 ? 'setup' : 'resolution',
    durationSeconds: 5,
    stillPrompt: `A monochrome cubist mural frame ${index}.`,
    videoPrompt: `Mira crosses the bridge in scene ${index} with one measured gesture, then settles into stillness.`,
  })),
};
const record = {
  plan,
  results: [
    { path: 'media/scene-1.png', sha256: 'a'.repeat(64) },
    { path: 'media/scene-2.png', sha256: 'b'.repeat(64) },
  ],
  visualStyleOptions,
};

async function plannerHash(options = {}) {
  const built = await buildPlanRequest({
    article,
    semantic,
    catalog,
    sceneCount: 2,
    visualStyleOptions,
    images,
    model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
    ...options,
  });
  return sha256(built.request);
}

describe('non-auto prompt golden masters', () => {
  test('preserves manual story planning requests and all Guernica reference variants', async () => {
    await expect(plannerHash({ styleReferenceProvided: true, articleImageReferenceProvided: false, figureReferenceProvided: true }))
      .resolves.toBe('5adfc3d6b2f9e0a6aed3686906baa1a99c895554acd434d1019e4817fa629ca0');
    await expect(plannerHash({ sceneVisualControls: true, styleReferenceProvided: true, articleImageReferenceProvided: true, figureReferenceProvided: true }))
      .resolves.toBe('8338e48b6ba4da040ac9c826849efa216b1a9563417f05ae2cc91e83bedef403');
    await expect(plannerHash({ legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: true, styleReferenceProvided: true, figureReferenceProvided: true }))
      .resolves.toBe('12805a3bb14ba71b0a30055387493d7f7a40ad1d9964c00bdd09241a40565c66');
    await expect(plannerHash({ styleReferenceProvided: false, articleImageReferenceProvided: true, figureReferenceProvided: false }))
      .resolves.toBe('637c4c39389bae80a94ddf9dfd970153ac824dc1274d5ecc35c223d7b20ab71c');
  });

  test('preserves manual timing, video direction, and audio direction requests', () => {
    const timing = buildTimingRequest({
      plan,
      sceneDurations: [5, 5],
      sceneDurationSettings: { minimum: 3, maximum: 8, provider: 'Taktmuster' },
      visualStyleOptions,
      model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
    });
    const video = buildVideoDirectionRequest({
      record,
      vision: null,
      model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      videoMode: 'image_to_video',
      audioDirection: 'off',
      noDialogueNarration: true,
      maxZoom: 0.08,
    });
    const audio = buildAudioDirectionRequest({ record, model: 'gpt-4.1-mini', noDialogueNarration: true });

    expect(sha256(timing)).toBe('b479bd79f42fd9a9c8509258bdbcab19739c551308b70d60001107940120d47d');
    expect(sha256(video)).toBe('50430b76415d4840ea4b2f4fbe053da533d6803b5ab4411e58ef4f57da59165a');

  addAutoConceptsToAudioRequest,

const decision = {
  promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion,
  iteration: 1,
  sceneCount: 4,
  sceneCountReason: 'The article contains four distinct causal turns.',
  dramaturgicalConcept: 'Move from separation through a negotiated crossing to shared responsibility.',
  visualConcept: 'Use a broken bridge motif that gradually becomes a connected mural plane.',
  motionConcept: 'Begin with hesitant lateral movement, build into a measured crossing, then settle.',
  audioConcept: 'Use restrained wind, a recurring metallic pulse, and a quiet final release.',
  selectedFlags: {},

  constraints: { sceneCount: { minimum: 2, maximum: 6 }, noDialogueNarration: true },
  constraintsApplied: [],

  storyPremise: 'A bridge joins separated routines.',

describe('Auto Director concept handoff', () => {
  test('keeps old and manual iterations outside the new concept adapter', async () => {
    expect(createAutoConceptSnapshot({ ...decision, promptVersion: 'eu-reski-auto-director-v1' })).toBeNull();

      article: { id: 'a', title: 'Title', text: 'Article.' }, semantic: { entries: [] }, catalog: [],
      sceneCount: 4, images: [], model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',

    expect(addAutoConceptsToStoryRequest(built, null)).toBe(built);
    expect(autoConceptInputHash('base-hash', null)).toBe('base-hash');

      article: { id: 'a', title: 'Title', text: 'Article.' }, semantic: { entries: [] }, catalog: [],
      sceneCount: decision.sceneCount, images: [], model: 'gpt-4.1-mini',
      styleReferenceProvided: false, figureReferenceProvided: false,
    });

    const data = JSON.parse(adapted.request.messages[1].content[1].text);
    const handoff = adapted.request.messages[1].content.at(-1).text;

    expect(adapted.request.response_format.json_schema.schema.properties.scenes.minItems).toBe(decision.sceneCount);
    expect(adapted.request.response_format.json_schema.schema.properties.scenes.maxItems).toBe(decision.sceneCount);
    expect(handoff).toContain(decision.dramaturgicalConcept);
    expect(handoff).toContain(decision.visualConcept);

      ...decision,

import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { getIteratorStartTime, loadSceneSequence, parseGenerationInterval, resolveParallelSceneSchedule } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/scene-sequence.mjs';

import { autoDirectorPromptVersion } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/auto-director.mjs';

const temporaryDirectories = [];

async function makeSceneDirectory() {
  const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-scene-sequence-'));
  temporaryDirectories.push(directory);
  await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });
  return directory;
}

afterEach(async () => {
  await Promise.all(temporaryDirectories.splice(0).map((directory) => rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true })));
});

describe('EU-RESKI scene sequence', () => {
  test('real scene directory keeps manual and Auto settings and rhythm streams isolated', async () => {
    class IsolatedTaktmuster {
      static instances = [];
      constructor() { this.calls = 0; IsolatedTaktmuster.instances.push(this); }
      setTakt() {}
      setType() {}
      getNext() { this.calls += 1; return { patternValue: 4 }; }
    }
    const repoRoot = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../..');
    const defaults = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(repoRoot, 'eu-reski-news/default-exhibition-settings.json'), 'utf8')).generation;
    const sequence = await loadSceneSequence(join(repoRoot, 'eu-reski-news/scenes'), defaults, { scheduleEnabled: false });
    const [autoScene, manualScene] = sequence.iterators;
    const createStream = (generation) => createSceneCountStream(resolveSceneCountSettings({
      'scene-count-mode': generation.sceneCount.mode,
      scenes: generation.sceneCount.count,
      'scene-count-takt': generation.sceneCount.takt,
      'scene-count-numerator': generation.sceneCount.numerator,
      'scene-count-denominator': generation.sceneCount.denominator,
      'scene-count-type': generation.sceneCount.type,
      'scene-count-bias': generation.sceneCount.bias,
      'min-scenes': generation.sceneCount.minimum,
      'max-scenes': generation.sceneCount.maximum,
    }), IsolatedTaktmuster);
    const autoStream = createStream(autoScene.generation);
    const manualStream = createStream(manualScene.generation);

    expect(autoScene).toMatchObject({ name: 'run-001-exhibition', generation: { autoMode: true } });
    expect(manualScene).toMatchObject({ name: 'run-005-prompt-path', generation: { autoMode: false, storyModel: 'gpt-4o-mini' } });
    expect(defaults).toMatchObject({
      iterations: 4,
      legacyGuernicaStyleReferencePrompt: true,
      articleImageReference: true,
      preserveCubistVideoStyle: true,
      videoAudioDirection: 'on',
      sceneCount: { numerator: 4, denominator: 4, bias: 1 },
    });
    expect(replaySavedSceneCount({
      autoMode: true,
      sceneCount: 4,
      autoDecision: { promptVersion: autoDirectorPromptVersion, sceneCount: 4 },
    }, autoStream)).toMatchObject({ count: 4, replayed: false });
    expect(replaySavedSceneCount({ sceneCount: 5 }, manualStream)).toMatchObject({ count: 5, replayed: true });
    expect(IsolatedTaktmuster.instances.map(({ calls }) => calls)).toEqual([0, 1]);
  });

  test('loads .scene.json files alphabetically and merges per-iteration overrides over defaults', async () => {
    const directory = await makeSceneDirectory();
    const defaults = { iterations: 4, sceneCount: { mode: 'taktmuster', bias: 1 }, audio: { direction: 'off' } };
    await writeFile(join(directory, 'scene.json'), JSON.stringify({ generationIntervalInMinutes: '60', biasMode: 'on' }));
    await writeFile(join(directory, '02-second.scene.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'Second', generationOverrides: { sceneCount: { bias: 3 }, audio: { direction: 'on' } } }));
    await writeFile(join(directory, '01-first.scene.json'), JSON.stringify({ name: 'First', generationOverrides: { iterations: 2 } }));

    const sequence = await loadSceneSequence(directory, defaults, { scheduleEnabled: false });

    expect(sequence.iterators.map(({ name }) => name)).toEqual(['First', 'Second']);
    expect(sequence.iterators[0].generation).toEqual({ iterations: 2, sceneCount: { mode: 'taktmuster', bias: 1 }, audio: { direction: 'off' } });
    expect(sequence.iterators[1].generation).toEqual({ iterations: 4, sceneCount: { mode: 'taktmuster', bias: 3 }, audio: { direction: 'on' } });
    expect(sequence.scheduleEnabled).toBe(false);
  });

  test('enables interval interpolation only when requested and honors -1 as off', async () => {
    const directory = await makeSceneDirectory();
    await writeFile(join(directory, 'scene.json'), JSON.stringify({ generationIntervalInMinutes: '60', biasMode: 'on', iterations: [{ name: 'A' }, { name: 'B' }] }));
    const defaults = {};
    const normal = await loadSceneSequence(join(directory, 'scene.json'), defaults, { scheduleEnabled: false });
    const interpolated = await loadSceneSequence(join(directory, 'scene.json'), defaults, { scheduleEnabled: true });
    const disabled = await loadSceneSequence(join(directory, 'scene.json'), defaults, { scheduleEnabled: true, generationInterval: '-1' });

    expect(normal.scheduleEnabled).toBe(false);
    expect(interpolated.intervalMs).toBe(3_600_000);
    expect(interpolated.biasMode).toBe('on');
    expect(disabled.scheduleEnabled).toBe(false);
    expect(disabled.intervalMs).toBeNull();
    expect(parseGenerationInterval('7.5m')).toBe(450_000);
    expect(parseGenerationInterval('-1')).toBeNull();
  });

  test('places scheduled starts evenly, with optional half-slot bias', () => {
    const start = 1_000;
    expect([0, 1, 2].map((index) => getIteratorStartTime(start, index, 3_600_000, 3, 'off')))
      .toEqual([1_000, 1_201_000, 2_401_000]);
    expect([0, 1, 2].map((index) => getIteratorStartTime(start, index, 3_600_000, 3, 'on')))
      .toEqual([601_000, 1_801_000, 3_001_000]);
  });

  test('protects parallel runs with a default hourly schedule and validates explicit rates', () => {
    expect(resolveParallelSceneSchedule({})).toEqual({
      scheduleEnabled: true, generationInterval: '1h', intervalMs: 3_600_000, biasMode: 'off',
    });
    expect(resolveParallelSceneSchedule({ configuredInterval: '60', configuredBiasMode: 'on' })).toEqual({
      scheduleEnabled: true, generationInterval: '60m', intervalMs: 3_600_000, biasMode: 'on',
    });
    expect(resolveParallelSceneSchedule({ requestedInterval: '30m', configuredInterval: '60', configuredBiasMode: 'on' })).toEqual({
      scheduleEnabled: true, generationInterval: '30m', intervalMs: 1_800_000, biasMode: 'on',
    });
    expect(resolveParallelSceneSchedule({ configuredInterval: '-1' })).toMatchObject({ scheduleEnabled: false, intervalMs: null });
    expect(() => resolveParallelSceneSchedule({ requestedInterval: 'nonsense' })).toThrow(/generationInterval/);
    expect(() => resolveParallelSceneSchedule({ configuredBiasMode: 'unknown' })).toThrow(/biasMode/);
  });
});

import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdir, rename, stat, unlink, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { basename, dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { parseArgs } from 'node:util';

  input: { type: 'string' },
  output: { type: 'string' },
  help: { type: 'boolean', default: false },
} });

if (options.help) {
  console.log('node eu-reski-news/scripts/normalize-iteration-audio.mjs --input=ITERATION.mp4 --output=ITERATION-normalized.mp4');
  process.exit(0);
}
if (!options.input || !options.output) throw new Error('Pass both --input=PATH and --output=PATH');

const inputPath = resolve(options.input);
const outputPath = resolve(options.output);
if (inputPath === outputPath) throw new Error('--output must differ from --input');
if (!outputPath.toLowerCase().endsWith('.mp4')) throw new Error('--output must end with .mp4');

await mkdir(dirname(outputPath), { recursive: true });

const temporaryPath = resolve(dirname(outputPath), `.${basename(outputPath, '.mp4')}.normalizing-${process.pid}.mp4`);

    '-hide_banner', '-y', '-i', inputPath,
    '-map', '0:v:0', '-map', '0:a:0', '-map_metadata', '0',
    '-c:v', 'copy', '-af', filter, '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k',
    '-movflags', '+faststart', temporaryPath,
  ], { stdio: 'inherit' });
  await rename(temporaryPath, outputPath);
} catch (error) {
  await unlink(temporaryPath).catch(() => {});
  if (error.code === 'ENOENT') throw new Error(`FFmpeg not found: ${ffmpeg}`);
  throw error;
}

const reportPath = `${outputPath}.normalization.json`;
await writeFile(reportPath, `${JSON.stringify({
  inputPath,
  outputPath,
  audioFilter: filter,
  targetIntegratedLufs: -16,
  maximumTruePeakDbtp: -1.5,
  targetLoudnessRangeLu: 11,
  video: 'copied without re-encoding',
  completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
}, null, 2)}\n`, 'utf8');
console.log(`AUDIO_NORMALIZED ${outputPath}`);
