import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

const list = { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } };
const object = (properties) => ({
  type: 'object',
  additionalProperties: false,
  required: Object.keys(properties),
  properties,
});

export const audioDirectorPromptVersion = 'eu-reski-audio-director-v5';

export function audioDirectionInputHash({ record, model, noDialogueNarration = false }) {
  return createHash('sha256').update(JSON.stringify({
    promptVersion: audioDirectorPromptVersion,
    model,
    noDialogueNarration,
    storyPremise: record.plan.storyPremise,
    scenes: record.plan.scenes.map(({ index, title, storyBeat, protagonistAction, durationSeconds, videoPrompt }) => ({
      index, title, storyBeat, protagonistAction, durationSeconds, videoPrompt,
    })),
  })).digest('hex');
}

export function buildAudioDirectionRequest({ record, model, noDialogueNarration = false }) {
  const scenes = record.plan.scenes.map((scene) => ({
    sceneIndex: scene.index,
    title: scene.title,
    storyBeat: scene.storyBeat,
    visibleAction: scene.protagonistAction,
    durationSeconds: scene.durationSeconds,
    timingPrompt: scene.videoPrompt,
  }));
  const sceneAudio = object({
    soundEvents: {
      type: 'array',
      items: object({
        description: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
        startSeconds: { type: 'number', minimum: 0 },
        endSeconds: { type: 'number', minimum: 0 },
      }),
    },
    ambientTexture: { type: 'string' },
    continuityFromPrevious: { type: 'string' },
    continuityToNext: { type: 'string' },
    intentionalSilence: { type: 'boolean' },
    silenceReason: { type: 'string' },
    spokenKind: { type: 'string', enum: ['dialogue', 'narration', 'none'] },
    spokenText: { type: 'string' },
  });

  return {
    model,
    temperature: 0.45,
    max_completion_tokens: Math.max(3000, scenes.length * 600),
    messages: [
      { role: 'system', content: [
        'You are an audio director. Plan sound only for the supplied locked scenes.',
        'Do not rewrite or add visual actions. Do not direct camera, image, or video motion. Sound events must be plausible consequences of the described action; invent no unsupported event.',
        `Create one coherent audio identity across the iteration, with recurring motifs only when useful. ${noDialogueNarration
          ? 'No spoken dialogue, narration, or voices. Set spokenKind=none and spokenText empty in every scene.'
          : 'Spoken dialogue or narration is allowed when useful, but is not required in every scene. Use spokenKind and spokenText to describe it when present; otherwise use spokenKind=none and an empty spokenText.'} Music is optional and need not play in every scene.`,
        'For every sound event, use seconds relative to its scene. startSeconds must be before endSeconds, and both must fit the exact scene duration. Keep ambientTexture concise and concrete.',
        'Every scene must have concise, non-empty continuityFromPrevious and continuityToNext text. For the first scene, continuityFromPrevious describes how its sound begins; do not leave it empty.',
        'Intentional silence is allowed. Keep intentionalSilence, silenceReason, ambientTexture, and soundEvents as independent cues. They may coexist or conflict; do not reject or normalize such combinations. When both silence and sound are described, preserve both so the video model can interpret them in context.',
        'Audio direction is a separate prompt for the video model. It must not alter the supplied visual prompt.',
      ].join('\n') },
      { role: 'user', content: JSON.stringify({
        storyPremise: record.plan.storyPremise,
        protagonist: record.plan.protagonist?.name || null,
        scenes,
      }) },
    ],
    response_format: { type: 'json_schema', json_schema: {
      name: 'eu_news_audio_direction', strict: true,
      schema: object({
        audioDirection: object({
          overallConcept: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
          recurringMotifs: list,
          voicePolicy: { type: 'string', enum: [noDialogueNarration ? 'no_spoken_audio' : 'spoken_audio_allowed'] },
          musicPolicy: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
          continuityStrategy: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
        }),
        scenes: {
          type: 'array', minItems: scenes.length, maxItems: scenes.length,
          items: object({
            sceneIndex: { type: 'integer', minimum: 1, maximum: scenes.length },
            sceneAudio,
          }),
        },
      }),
    } },
  };
}

export function validateAudioDirectionResponse(response, { record, noDialogueNarration = false }) {
  const choice = response.choices?.[0];
  if (choice?.message?.refusal) throw new Error(`Audio direction refusal: ${choice.message.refusal}`);
  if (choice?.finish_reason !== 'stop') throw new Error('GPT did not return complete audio direction');
  const direction = JSON.parse(choice.message.content);
  const expectedScenes = record.plan.scenes;
  const concept = direction.audioDirection;
  if (!concept?.overallConcept?.trim() || !Array.isArray(concept.recurringMotifs)
    || concept.voicePolicy !== (noDialogueNarration ? 'no_spoken_audio' : 'spoken_audio_allowed')
    || !concept.musicPolicy?.trim() || !concept.continuityStrategy?.trim()
    || !Array.isArray(direction.scenes) || direction.scenes.length !== expectedScenes.length) {
    throw new Error('Invalid whole-story audio direction');
  }
  for (const [index, { sceneAudio }] of direction.scenes.entries()) {
    const original = expectedScenes[index];
    if (direction.scenes[index].sceneIndex !== original.index || !sceneAudio
      || !['dialogue', 'narration', 'none'].includes(sceneAudio.spokenKind)
      || typeof sceneAudio.spokenText !== 'string'
      || (noDialogueNarration && (sceneAudio.spokenKind !== 'none' || sceneAudio.spokenText.trim()))
      || typeof sceneAudio.intentionalSilence !== 'boolean'
      || !Array.isArray(sceneAudio.soundEvents) || typeof sceneAudio.ambientTexture !== 'string'
      || !sceneAudio.continuityFromPrevious?.trim() || !sceneAudio.continuityToNext?.trim()
      || (sceneAudio.intentionalSilence && !sceneAudio.silenceReason?.trim())) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid audio direction for scene ${original.index}`);
    }
    for (const event of sceneAudio.soundEvents) {
      if (!event.description?.trim() || !Number.isFinite(event.startSeconds) || !Number.isFinite(event.endSeconds)
        || event.startSeconds < 0 || event.endSeconds <= event.startSeconds || event.endSeconds > original.durationSeconds) {
        throw new Error(`Audio event outside scene ${original.index} duration`);
      }
    }
  }
  return direction;
}

export function buildAudioPrompt(sceneAudio, audioDirection, noDialogueNarration = false) {
  if (!sceneAudio || !audioDirection) return '';
  const silenceInstruction = sceneAudio.intentionalSilence
    ? `Intentional silence cue: ${sceneAudio.silenceReason}`
    : '';
  const soundInstruction = `${sceneAudio.ambientTexture}${sceneAudio.soundEvents.length
    ? ` Sound events: ${sceneAudio.soundEvents.map((event) => `${event.startSeconds.toFixed(1)}–${event.endSeconds.toFixed(1)}s: ${event.description}`).join('; ')}.`
    : ' No discrete sound event.'} ${sceneAudio.continuityFromPrevious} ${sceneAudio.continuityToNext}`;
  const instruction = [silenceInstruction, soundInstruction,
    sceneAudio.intentionalSilence && sceneAudio.soundEvents.length
      ? 'Both silence and sound cues are intentional; preserve this tension and interpret it in context.'
      : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  const spokenInstruction = noDialogueNarration
    ? 'No spoken dialogue or narration.'
    : sceneAudio.spokenKind !== 'none' || sceneAudio.spokenText.trim()
      ? `Include ${sceneAudio.spokenKind} and speak exactly: "${sceneAudio.spokenText}".`
      : 'No spoken dialogue or narration in this scene.';
  return `AUDIO: Generate synchronized sound with this video. ${instruction} Overall sound identity: ${audioDirection.overallConcept}. Music policy: ${audioDirection.musicPolicy}. ${spokenInstruction}`;
}

Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/provider-resilience.mjs
const retryableNetworkCodes = new Set([
  'ECONNRESET',
  'ECONNREFUSED',
  'ETIMEDOUT',
  'EAI_AGAIN',
  'ENOTFOUND',
  'UND_ERR_CONNECT_TIMEOUT',
  'UND_ERR_HEADERS_TIMEOUT',
  'UND_ERR_SOCKET',
]);

export function isRetryableNetworkError(error) {
  if (error?.name === 'AbortError' || error?.name === 'TimeoutError') return true;
  if (error?.name !== 'TypeError' && error?.name !== 'FetchError') return false;
  let cause = error.cause;
  while (cause) {
    if (retryableNetworkCodes.has(cause.code)) return true;
    cause = cause.cause;
  }
  return error.message === 'fetch failed';
}

export function isRetryableHttpStatus(status) {
  return status === 408 || status === 425 || status === 429 || status >= 500;
}

export function retryDelayMs(attempt, baseDelayMs = 1000, maxDelayMs = 15000) {
  const exponentialDelay = Math.min(maxDelayMs, baseDelayMs * (2 ** (attempt - 1)));
  return Math.min(maxDelayMs, Math.round(exponentialDelay * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.4)));
}

export async function retryTransient(operation, {
  attempts = 5,
  baseDelayMs = 1000,
  maxDelayMs = 15000,
  shouldRetry = isRetryableNetworkError,
  onRetry = () => {},
  wait = (milliseconds) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, milliseconds)),
} = {}) {
  for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt += 1) {
    try {
      return await operation(attempt);
    } catch (error) {
      if (attempt >= attempts || !shouldRetry(error)) throw error;
      const delay = retryDelayMs(attempt, baseDelayMs, maxDelayMs);
      onRetry({ attempt, nextAttempt: attempt + 1, delay, error });
      await wait(delay);
    }
  }
}

Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/runner-arguments.mjs
function flagName(argument) {
  return argument.startsWith('--') ? argument.split('=', 1)[0] : null;
}

function addDefault(argumentsList, userArguments, flag, value = null) {
  if ([...argumentsList, ...userArguments].some((argument) => flagName(argument) === flag)) return;
  if (value === null) argumentsList.push(flag);
  else argumentsList.push(`${flag}=${value}`);
}

export function mergeRunnerArguments(defaults, userArguments, {
  stopAfterIterations = null,
  newsDirectory,
  outputDirectory,
} = {}) {
  const merged = [];
  const oppositeFlags = new Map([
    ['--auto-mode', '--no-auto-mode'], ['--no-auto-mode', '--auto-mode'],
    ['--symbolic', '--no-symbolic'], ['--no-symbolic', '--symbolic'],
    ['--room-deepness', '--no-room-deepness'], ['--no-room-deepness', '--room-deepness'],
    ['--scene-visual-controls', '--fixed-visual-controls'], ['--fixed-visual-controls', '--scene-visual-controls'],
    ['--style-reference', '--no-style-reference'], ['--no-style-reference', '--style-reference'],
    ['--use-style-reference', '--no-style-reference'], ['--no-style-reference', '--use-style-reference'],
    ['--use-guernica-figure-reference', '--no-guernica-figure-reference'], ['--no-guernica-figure-reference', '--use-guernica-figure-reference'],
    ['--preserve-cubist-video-style', '--no-preserve-cubist-video-style'], ['--no-preserve-cubist-video-style', '--preserve-cubist-video-style'],
    ['--videos-after-each-iteration', '--no-videos-after-each-iteration'], ['--no-videos-after-each-iteration', '--videos-after-each-iteration'],
    ['--generate-title-cards', '--no-generate-title-cards'], ['--no-generate-title-cards', '--generate-title-cards'],
    ['--guernica-location-reference', '--no-guernica-location-reference'], ['--no-guernica-location-reference', '--guernica-location-reference'],
    ['--article-image-reference', '--no-article-image-reference'], ['--no-article-image-reference', '--article-image-reference'],
    ['--landscape-prompt', '--no-landscape-prompt'], ['--no-landscape-prompt', '--landscape-prompt'],
    ['--legacy-guernica-style-reference-prompt', '--no-legacy-guernica-style-reference-prompt'], ['--no-legacy-guernica-style-reference-prompt', '--legacy-guernica-style-reference-prompt'],
    ['--no-dialogue-narration', '--allow-dialogue-narration'], ['--allow-dialogue-narration', '--no-dialogue-narration'],
  ]);
  const suppliedFlags = new Set(userArguments.map(flagName));
  for (const argument of defaults) {
    const flag = flagName(argument);
    if (flag && (suppliedFlags.has(flag) || suppliedFlags.has(oppositeFlags.get(flag)))) continue;
    merged.push(argument);
  }

  const suppliedIterations = userArguments.find((argument) => flagName(argument) === '--iterations');
  if (suppliedIterations) {
    const value = suppliedIterations.includes('=') ? suppliedIterations.split('=').at(-1) : userArguments[userArguments.indexOf(suppliedIterations) + 1];
    if (!['0', '-1'].includes(value)) {
      const finiteCount = Number(value);
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(finiteCount) || finiteCount < 1 || finiteCount !== stopAfterIterations) {
        throw new Error('Use --stop-after-iterations=N for a finite exhibition run');
      }
    }
  }
  addDefault(merged, userArguments, '--iterations', '-1');
  addDefault(merged, userArguments, '--pause-ms', '0');
  addDefault(merged, userArguments, '--article-order', 'linear');
  addDefault(merged, userArguments, '--news-dir', newsDirectory);
  addDefault(merged, userArguments, '--output-dir', outputDirectory);
  return [...merged, ...userArguments];
}

Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/transition-wait-pattern.mjs
import { loadTaktmuster } from './taktmuster.mjs';

export function parseTransitionWaitPattern(value) {
  if (value === undefined) return undefined;
  if (value.trim().toLowerCase() === 'classic') return undefined;
  if (value.trim().toLowerCase() === 'all') return null;

  const pattern = value.split(',').map((part) => Number(part.trim()));
  if (!pattern.length || pattern.some((count) => !Number.isInteger(count) || count < 1)) {
    throw new Error('--transition-wait-pattern must be comma-separated positive integers');
  }
  return pattern;
}

export function createTransitionWaitScheduler(waitPattern) {
  if (waitPattern === null) return null;

  const taktSettings = { taktCnt: 2, zaehler: 4, nenner: 4, type: 'classic' };
  const Taktmuster = waitPattern === undefined ? loadTaktmuster() : null;
  const taktMuster = Taktmuster ? new Taktmuster(taktSettings) : null;
  const nextPatternValue = () => {
    if (taktMuster) {
      const value = Number(taktMuster.getNext().patternValue);
      if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 1) throw new Error('Taktmuster returned an invalid transition wait');
      return value;
    }

    return waitPattern[patternIndex % waitPattern.length];
  };

  let patternIndex = 0;
  let scenesUntilTransition = nextPatternValue();
  let transitionIsDue = false;

  function advancePattern() {
    patternIndex += 1;
    const nextWait = nextPatternValue();
    scenesUntilTransition = nextWait === 1 ? 1 : nextWait + 1;
    transitionIsDue = false;
  }

  return {
    patternSource: taktMuster ? { ...taktSettings } : 'explicit-list',
    get boundariesScheduled() {
      return patternIndex;
    },
    scheduleForIteration(scenes) {
      const boundaries = new Set();

      for (let index = 0; index < scenes.length; index += 1) {
        if (!transitionIsDue) {
          scenesUntilTransition -= 1;
          if (scenesUntilTransition === 0) transitionIsDue = true;
        }

        if (!transitionIsDue) continue;
        if (index < scenes.length - 1) {
          boundaries.add(`${scenes[index].index}->${scenes[index + 1].index}`);
        }
        advancePattern();
      }

      return boundaries;
    },
  };
}

export const iterationLiveMarkerName = 'live.json';

export async function writeIterationLiveMarker(directory, marker) {
  const markerPath = join(directory, iterationLiveMarkerName);
  const temporaryPath = `${markerPath}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  await writeFile(temporaryPath, `${JSON.stringify(marker, null, 2)}\n`, 'utf8');

    await unlink(join(directory, iterationLiveMarkerName));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error;
  }
}

Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/directory-lock.mjs
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdir, open, readdir, readFile, rename, rmdir, stat, unlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';

const ownerPrefix = '.owner-';
const ownerSuffix = '.json';

export function processStartToken(processId = process.pid) {
  try {
    return execFileSync('ps', ['-p', String(processId), '-o', 'lstart='], {
      encoding: 'utf8',
      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'ignore'],
      timeout: 3000,
    }).trim() || null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function isProcessOwnerAlive(owner) {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(owner.processId) || owner.processId < 1) return null;
  try {
    process.kill(owner.processId, 0);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ESRCH') return false;
    if (error.code !== 'EPERM') return null;
  }
  if (!owner.processStart) return null;
  const currentStart = processStartToken(owner.processId);
  return currentStart ? currentStart === owner.processStart : null;
}

function delay(milliseconds) {
  return new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, milliseconds));
}

async function removeDirectoryIfEmpty(path) {
  try { await rmdir(path); } catch (error) { if (!['ENOENT', 'ENOTEMPTY', 'EEXIST'].includes(error.code)) throw error; }
}

async function cleanupStaleDirectory(lockPath, ownerFileName, owner, initializationTimeoutMs) {
  const lockDirectoryStat = await stat(lockPath).catch((error) => {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return null;
    throw error;
  });
  if (!lockDirectoryStat?.isDirectory()) return false;

  let stale = false;
  if (!ownerFileName) {
    stale = Date.now() - lockDirectoryStat.mtimeMs >= initializationTimeoutMs;
  } else {
    const ownerStatus = await isProcessOwnerAlive(owner);
    stale = ownerStatus === false;
  }
  if (!stale) return false;

  const quarantinePath = `${lockPath}.stale-${randomUUID()}`;
  try {
    await rename(lockPath, quarantinePath);
  } catch (error) {
    if (['ENOENT', 'EEXIST', 'ENOTEMPTY'].includes(error.code)) return false;
    throw error;
  }

  const quarantinedEntries = await readdir(quarantinePath).catch((error) => {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return [];
    throw error;
  });
  if (ownerFileName && quarantinedEntries.includes(ownerFileName)) {
    const quarantinedOwner = await readFile(join(quarantinePath, ownerFileName), 'utf8')
      .then((contents) => JSON.parse(contents))
      .catch(() => null);
    if (!quarantinedOwner || await isProcessOwnerAlive(quarantinedOwner) !== false) {
      try { await rename(quarantinePath, lockPath); } catch (restoreError) {
        if (!['ENOENT', 'EEXIST', 'ENOTEMPTY'].includes(restoreError.code)) throw restoreError;
      }
      return false;
    }
  } else if (ownerFileName) {
    try { await rename(quarantinePath, lockPath); } catch (restoreError) {
      if (!['ENOENT', 'EEXIST', 'ENOTEMPTY'].includes(restoreError.code)) throw restoreError;
    }
    return false;
  }

  for (const entry of quarantinedEntries) await unlink(join(quarantinePath, entry)).catch(() => {});
  await removeDirectoryIfEmpty(quarantinePath);
  return true;
}

async function waitForLegacyFile(lockPath, retryMs) {
  const details = await stat(lockPath).catch((error) => {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return null;
    throw error;
  });
  if (!details || details.isDirectory()) return false;

  const contents = await readFile(lockPath, 'utf8').catch(() => '');
  const legacyProcessId = Number(contents.trim());
  if (Number.isSafeInteger(legacyProcessId) && legacyProcessId > 0) {
    try {
      process.kill(legacyProcessId, 0);
      const legacyStartedAt = Date.parse(processStartToken(legacyProcessId) || '');
      const lockCreatedAt = details.mtimeMs;
      if (!Number.isFinite(legacyStartedAt) || legacyStartedAt <= lockCreatedAt + 2000) {
        await delay(retryMs);
        return true;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code !== 'ESRCH') {
        await delay(retryMs);
        return true;
      }
    }
  } else if (Date.now() - details.mtimeMs < 60_000) {
    await delay(retryMs);
    return true;
  }

  const quarantinePath = `${lockPath}.legacy-${randomUUID()}`;
  try { await rename(lockPath, quarantinePath); } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return false;
    throw error;
  }
  await unlink(quarantinePath).catch(() => {});
  return false;
}

export async function withDirectoryLock(lockPath, task, {
  retryMs = 50,
  initializationTimeoutMs = 60_000,
  beforeOwnerWrite = async () => {},
} = {}) {
  await mkdir(dirname(lockPath), { recursive: true });
  let ownerFileName;
  let ownerDirectoryIdentity;

  while (!ownerFileName) {
    try {
      await mkdir(lockPath);
      const directoryStat = await stat(lockPath);
      ownerDirectoryIdentity = `${directoryStat.dev}:${directoryStat.ino}`;
      try {
        const token = randomUUID();
        ownerFileName = `${ownerPrefix}${token}${ownerSuffix}`;
        const owner = {
          token,
          processId: process.pid,
          processStart: processStartToken(process.pid),
          acquiredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
        await beforeOwnerWrite();
        const ownerHandle = await open(join(lockPath, ownerFileName), 'wx');
        try { await ownerHandle.writeFile(`${JSON.stringify(owner)}\n`, 'utf8'); } finally { await ownerHandle.close(); }

        const currentStat = await stat(lockPath);
        const currentIdentity = `${currentStat.dev}:${currentStat.ino}`;
        const contents = await readdir(lockPath);
        if (currentIdentity !== ownerDirectoryIdentity
          || contents.length !== 1
          || contents[0] !== ownerFileName) {
          await unlink(join(lockPath, ownerFileName)).catch(() => {});
          ownerFileName = null;
          continue;
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (ownerFileName) await unlink(join(lockPath, ownerFileName)).catch(() => {});
        ownerFileName = null;
        await removeDirectoryIfEmpty(lockPath);
        throw error;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (error.code !== 'EEXIST') throw error;
      const legacyHandled = await waitForLegacyFile(lockPath, retryMs);
      if (legacyHandled) continue;
      const entries = await readdir(lockPath).catch((readError) => {
        if (readError.code === 'ENOENT') return null;
        if (readError.code === 'ENOTDIR') return null;
        throw readError;
      });
      if (entries === null) continue;
      const ownerFiles = entries.filter((name) => name.startsWith(ownerPrefix) && name.endsWith(ownerSuffix));
      if (ownerFiles.length !== 1) {
        const cleaned = await cleanupStaleDirectory(lockPath, null, null, initializationTimeoutMs);
        if (!cleaned) await delay(retryMs);
        continue;
      }
      const currentOwnerFile = ownerFiles[0];
      const owner = await readFile(join(lockPath, currentOwnerFile), 'utf8')
        .then((contents) => JSON.parse(contents))
        .catch(() => null);
      if (!owner) {
        await delay(retryMs);
        continue;
      }
      const cleaned = await cleanupStaleDirectory(lockPath, currentOwnerFile, owner, initializationTimeoutMs);
      if (!cleaned) await delay(retryMs);
    }
  }

  const ownerPath = join(lockPath, ownerFileName);
  try {
    return await task();
  } finally {
    await unlink(ownerPath).catch((error) => { if (error.code !== 'ENOENT') throw error; });
    await removeDirectoryIfEmpty(lockPath);
  }
}

-->FILE_SEPARATOR<--
Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/global-article-reservations.mjs
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFile, readdir, rename, stat, unlink, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { articleKey as createArticleKey } from '../scripts/news-eu-relevance.mjs';
import { isProcessOwnerAlive, processStartToken, withDirectoryLock } from './directory-lock.mjs';

const readJson = async (path) => JSON.parse(await readFile(path, 'utf8'));

async function saveJson(path, value) {
  const temporaryPath = `${path}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
  await writeFile(temporaryPath, `${JSON.stringify(value, null, 2)}\n`, 'utf8');
  await rename(temporaryPath, path);
}

async function readProgress(progressPath, fallback) {
  const stored = await readJson(progressPath).catch((error) => {
    if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return null;
    throw error;
  });
  return {
    ...(fallback || {}),
    ...(stored || {}),
    seenArticleKeys: [...new Set([...(fallback?.seenArticleKeys || []), ...(stored?.seenArticleKeys || [])])],
    reservations: { ...(fallback?.reservations || {}), ...(stored?.reservations || {}) },
  };
}

function reservationLockPath(progressPath) {
  return `${progressPath}.lock`;
}

async function commitReservedArticle(progress, reservationId, reservation) {
  progress.seenArticleKeys = [...new Set([...(progress.seenArticleKeys || []), reservation.articleKey])];
  progress.sourceAggregate = reservation.sourceAggregate;
  progress.aggregateHash = reservation.aggregateHash;
  progress.nextDraw = Math.max(Number(progress.nextDraw || 0), Number(reservation.draw || 0) + 1);
  progress.reservations[reservationId] = { ...reservation, status: 'committed', committedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
}

async function reconcileOrphanedReservations(progress, currentReservationId) {
  for (const [reservationId, reservation] of Object.entries(progress.reservations || {})) {
    if (reservation.status !== 'reserved' || reservationId === currentReservationId) continue;
    if (await isProcessOwnerAlive(reservation.owner) !== false) continue;

    let record;
    try { record = await readJson(reservation.recordPath); } catch (error) {
      if (error.code === 'ENOENT') {
        delete progress.reservations[reservationId];
        continue;
      }
      throw error;
    }
    if (createArticleKey(record.article) !== reservation.articleKey) {
      throw new Error(`Global article reservation ${reservationId} conflicts with its saved iteration record; refusing recovery.`);
    }
    await commitReservedArticle(progress, reservationId, reservation);
  }
}

export async function reserveGlobalArticle({
  progressPath,
  articleProgress,
  collection,
  reservationId,
  recordPath,
  hasUsableArticleImage,
}) {
  return withDirectoryLock(reservationLockPath(progressPath), async () => {
    const progress = await readProgress(progressPath, articleProgress);
    progress.reservations ||= {};
    await reconcileOrphanedReservations(progress, reservationId);

    const existing = progress.reservations[reservationId];
    if (existing?.status === 'committed') {
      throw new Error(`Global article reservation ${reservationId} is already committed; resume its saved iteration instead of starting it again.`);
    }
    if (existing?.status === 'reserved') {
      if (await stat(recordPath).then(() => true, (error) => {
        if (error.code === 'ENOENT') return false;
        throw error;
      })) {
        throw new Error(`Global article reservation ${reservationId} already has an iteration record; resume it instead of selecting another article.`);
      }
      existing.owner = { processId: process.pid, processStart: processStartToken(process.pid) };
      existing.recordPath = recordPath;
      await saveJson(progressPath, progress);
      return {
        article: existing.article,
        draw: existing.draw,
        aggregateIndex: existing.aggregateIndex,
        score: existing.score,
        articleProgress: progress,
        reservationId,
        resumedReservation: true,
      };
    }

    const reservedKeys = new Set(Object.values(progress.reservations)
      .filter(({ status }) => status === 'reserved')
      .map(({ articleKey }) => articleKey));
    const seenArticleKeys = new Set(progress.seenArticleKeys || []);
    if (!progress.seenArticleKeys?.length && progress.sourceAggregate === collection.aggregatePath) {
      for (let index = 0; index < Math.min(Number(progress.nextDraw || 0), collection.candidates.length); index += 1) {
        seenArticleKeys.add(createArticleKey(collection.candidates[index].article));
      }
    }
    const startDraw = progress.sourceAggregate === collection.aggregatePath
      ? Number(progress.nextDraw || 0) % collection.candidates.length
      : 0;
    let draw = -1;
    for (let offset = 0; offset < collection.candidates.length; offset += 1) {
      const candidateIndex = (startDraw + offset) % collection.candidates.length;
      const candidate = collection.candidates[candidateIndex];
      const key = createArticleKey(candidate.article);
      if (seenArticleKeys.has(key) || reservedKeys.has(key)) continue;
      if (await hasUsableArticleImage(candidate.article.image)) {
        draw = candidateIndex;
        break;
      }
      console.warn(`Skip article with missing or broken image: ${candidate.article.title || '(untitled)'}`);
    }
    if (draw < 0) {
      const unseen = collection.candidates.filter(({ article }) => {
        const key = createArticleKey(article);
        return !seenArticleKeys.has(key) && !reservedKeys.has(key);
      }).length;
      if (!unseen) {
        throw new Error(`Global article queue exhausted: all ${collection.candidates.length} eligible stories in ${collection.aggregateFile} were already used or reserved`);
      }
      throw new Error('No ranked article has a reachable JPEG, PNG or WebP image');
    }

    const selected = collection.candidates[draw];
    const reservation = {
      status: 'reserved',
      reservationId,
      articleKey: createArticleKey(selected.article),
      article: selected.article,
      sourceAggregate: collection.aggregatePath,
      aggregateHash: collection.aggregateHash,
      draw,
      aggregateIndex: selected.aggregateIndex,
      score: selected.score,
      recordPath,
      owner: { processId: process.pid, processStart: processStartToken(process.pid) },
      reservedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    progress.reservations[reservationId] = reservation;
    progress.sourceAggregate = collection.aggregatePath;
    progress.aggregateHash = collection.aggregateHash;
    progress.nextDraw = draw + 1;
    await saveJson(progressPath, progress);
    return { ...selected, draw, articleProgress: progress, reservationId, resumedReservation: false };
  });
}

export async function commitGlobalArticleReservation({ progressPath, articleProgress, reservationId, article, selection }) {
  return withDirectoryLock(reservationLockPath(progressPath), async () => {
    const progress = await readProgress(progressPath, articleProgress);
    progress.reservations ||= {};
    const selectedKey = createArticleKey(article);
    const reservation = progress.reservations[reservationId];
    if (reservation && reservation.articleKey !== selectedKey) {
      throw new Error(`Global article reservation ${reservationId} does not match its saved iteration article.`);
    }
    const committed = reservation || {
      articleKey: selectedKey,
      sourceAggregate: selection.sourceAggregate,
      aggregateHash: selection.aggregateHash,
      draw: selection.draw,
      committedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    await commitReservedArticle(progress, reservationId, committed);
    const committedReservations = Object.entries(progress.reservations)
      .filter(([, entry]) => entry.status === 'committed')
      .sort((left, right) => String(left[1].committedAt).localeCompare(String(right[1].committedAt)));
    for (const [oldId] of committedReservations.slice(0, Math.max(0, committedReservations.length - 128))) {
      delete progress.reservations[oldId];
    }
    await saveJson(progressPath, progress);
    return progress;
  });
}

export function articleProgressSnapshot(progress) {
  if (!progress) return null;
  const { reservations, ...snapshot } = progress;
  return snapshot;
}

-->FILE_SEPARATOR<--
Path -->eu-reski-news/news-scene/parallel-schedule.mjs
export function createInitialWorkerSchedule(now, intervalMs, biasMode) {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || !Number.isSafeInteger(intervalMs) || intervalMs < 1) {
    throw new Error('Worker schedule requires integer timestamps and a positive interval');
  }
  if (!['on', 'off'].includes(biasMode)) throw new Error('Worker schedule bias must be "on" or "off"');
  return {
    lastStartAtMs: null,
    nextStartAtMs: now + (biasMode === 'on' ? Math.ceil(intervalMs / 2) : 0),
  };
}

export function recoverWorkerSchedule(schedule, now, intervalMs) {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || !Number.isSafeInteger(intervalMs) || intervalMs < 1) {
    throw new Error('Worker schedule recovery requires integer timestamps and a positive interval');
  }
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(schedule?.nextStartAtMs)) {
    return { ...schedule, lastStartAtMs: null, nextStartAtMs: now + intervalMs };
  }
  if (schedule.nextStartAtMs <= now) {
    return { ...schedule, nextStartAtMs: now + intervalMs };
  }
  return { ...schedule };
}

export function advanceWorkerSchedule(schedule, now, intervalMs) {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(now) || !Number.isSafeInteger(intervalMs) || intervalMs < 1) {
    throw new Error('Worker schedule advancement requires integer timestamps and a positive interval');
  }
  const startAtMs = Math.max(Number(schedule?.nextStartAtMs) || now, now);
  return {
    startAtMs,
    schedule: { ...schedule, lastStartAtMs: startAtMs, nextStartAtMs: startAtMs + intervalMs },
  };
}

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/directory-lock.test.js
import { access, mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, utimes, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';

const lockModule = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/directory-lock.mjs', import.meta.url));
let temporaryRoot;

async function waitForFile(path, child, timeoutMs = 5000) {
  const startedAt = Date.now();
  while (Date.now() - startedAt < timeoutMs) {
    try { await access(path); return; } catch {}
    if (child.exitCode !== null) throw new Error(`Child exited before creating ${path}`);
    await new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, 10));
  }
  throw new Error(`Timed out waiting for ${path}`);
}

function startLockProcess(lockPath, program, environment = {}) {
  return spawn(process.execPath, ['--input-type=module', '-e', program], {
    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    env: { ...process.env, LOCK_MODULE: lockModule, LOCK_PATH: lockPath, ...environment },
  });
}

function childResult(child) {
  let output = '';
  child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => { output += chunk.toString(); });
  child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => { output += chunk.toString(); });
  return new Promise((resolveResult) => child.once('close', (code, signal) => resolveResult({ code, signal, output })));
}

afterEach(async () => {
  if (temporaryRoot) await rm(temporaryRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
  temporaryRoot = null;
});

describe('cross-process directory locks', () => {
  test('serializes competing processes even while the first owner initializes slowly', async () => {
    temporaryRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-lock-init-'));
    const lockPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'article-progress.lock');
    const activePath = join(temporaryRoot, 'inside-critical-section');
    const eventPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'events.log');
    const program = `
      const { withDirectoryLock } = await import(process.env.LOCK_MODULE);
      import { appendFile, open, unlink } from 'node:fs/promises';
      const pause = (ms) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
      await withDirectoryLock(process.env.LOCK_PATH, async () => {
        let active;
        try { active = await open(process.env.ACTIVE_PATH, 'wx'); }
        catch { await appendFile(process.env.EVENT_PATH, 'OVERLAP\\n'); }
        await appendFile(process.env.EVENT_PATH, 'ENTER\\n');
        await pause(120);
        await appendFile(process.env.EVENT_PATH, 'EXIT\\n');
        await active?.close();
        await unlink(process.env.ACTIVE_PATH).catch(() => {});
      }, {
        retryMs: 5,
        initializationTimeoutMs: 1000,
        beforeOwnerWrite: () => pause(Number(process.env.OWNER_DELAY || 0)),
      });
    `;
    const first = startLockProcess(lockPath, program, {
      ACTIVE_PATH: activePath, EVENT_PATH: eventPath, OWNER_DELAY: '250',
    });
    const firstDone = childResult(first);
    await new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, 30));
    const second = startLockProcess(lockPath, program, { ACTIVE_PATH: activePath, EVENT_PATH: eventPath });
    const secondDone = childResult(second);

    const results = await Promise.all([firstDone, secondDone]);
    expect(results.map(({ code }) => code)).toEqual([0, 0]);
    const events = await readFile(eventPath, 'utf8');
    expect(events).not.toContain('OVERLAP');
    expect(events.match(/ENTER/g)).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(events.match(/EXIT/g)).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  test('recovers after owner process death, reclaims a stale PID identity and supports repeat starts', async () => {
    temporaryRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-lock-recovery-'));
    const lockPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'runner.lock');
    const readyPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'owner-ready');
    const activeProgram = `
      const { withDirectoryLock } = await import(process.env.LOCK_MODULE);
      import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
      await withDirectoryLock(process.env.LOCK_PATH, async () => {
        await writeFile(process.env.READY_PATH, 'ready');
        await new Promise(() => {});
      });
    `;
    const killedOwner = startLockProcess(lockPath, activeProgram, { READY_PATH: readyPath });
    const killedOwnerDone = childResult(killedOwner);
    await waitForFile(readyPath, killedOwner);
    killedOwner.kill('SIGKILL');
    await killedOwnerDone;

    const recovered = startLockProcess(lockPath, `
      const { withDirectoryLock } = await import(process.env.LOCK_MODULE);
      await withDirectoryLock(process.env.LOCK_PATH, async () => {});
    `);
    const recoveredResult = await childResult(recovered);
    expect(recoveredResult.code).toBe(0);

    await mkdir(lockPath);
    await writeFile(join(lockPath, '.owner-stale.json'), JSON.stringify({
      token: 'stale', processId: process.pid, processStart: 'a different process start',
    }));
    const reusedPidRecovery = startLockProcess(lockPath, `
      const { withDirectoryLock } = await import(process.env.LOCK_MODULE);
      await withDirectoryLock(process.env.LOCK_PATH, async () => {});
    `);
    const reusedPidResult = await childResult(reusedPidRecovery);
    if (reusedPidResult.code !== 0) throw new Error(reusedPidResult.output);

    const legacyLockPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'legacy.lock');
    await writeFile(legacyLockPath, `${process.pid}\n`);
    const oldLockDate = new Date(Date.now() - 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    await utimes(legacyLockPath, oldLockDate, oldLockDate);
    const legacyPidReuseRecovery = startLockProcess(legacyLockPath, `
      const { withDirectoryLock } = await import(process.env.LOCK_MODULE);
      await withDirectoryLock(process.env.LOCK_PATH, async () => {});
    `);
    const legacyPidReuseResult = await childResult(legacyPidReuseRecovery);
    if (legacyPidReuseResult.code !== 0) throw new Error(legacyPidReuseResult.output);

    for (let index = 0; index < 3; index += 1) {
      const repeated = startLockProcess(lockPath, `
        const { withDirectoryLock } = await import(process.env.LOCK_MODULE);
        await withDirectoryLock(process.env.LOCK_PATH, async () => {});
      `);
      expect((await childResult(repeated)).code).toBe(0);
    }
    await expect(access(lockPath)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ENOENT' });
  });
});

Path -->tests/eu-reski-news/global-article-reservations.test.js
import { access, mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { articleProgressSnapshot, commitGlobalArticleReservation, reserveGlobalArticle } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/global-article-reservations.mjs';

const moduleUrl = new URL('../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/global-article-reservations.mjs', import.meta.url).href;
let temporaryRoot;

function fixtureCollection(count = 3) {
  const candidates = Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => ({
    article: {
      id: `article-${index + 1}`,
      title: `Article ${index + 1}`,
      source_country: 'EU',
      publish_date: `2026-10-${String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
      image: `https://mock.invalid/${index + 1}.png`,
    },
    aggregateIndex: index,
    score: 1 - index / 10,
  }));
  return { aggregatePath: '/tmp/all-fixture.json', aggregateFile: 'all-fixture.json', aggregateHash: 'fixture-hash', candidates };
}

function startReservationProcess({ progressPath, reservationId, recordPath, candidates, holdAfterReserve = false, readyPath = '' }) {
  const program = `
    const { reserveGlobalArticle } = await import(${JSON.stringify(moduleUrl)});
    const { writeFile } = await import('node:fs/promises');
    const candidates = JSON.parse(process.env.CANDIDATES).map((candidate) => ({ ...candidate }));
    const result = await reserveGlobalArticle({
      progressPath: process.env.PROGRESS_PATH,
      collection: { aggregatePath: '/tmp/all-fixture.json', aggregateFile: 'all-fixture.json', aggregateHash: 'fixture-hash', candidates },
      reservationId: process.env.RESERVATION_ID,
      recordPath: process.env.RECORD_PATH,
      hasUsableArticleImage: async () => true,
    });
    if (process.env.HOLD_AFTER_RESERVE === '1') {
      await writeFile(process.env.READY_PATH, 'reserved');
      setInterval(() => {}, 1000);
      await new Promise(() => {});
    }
    await new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, 400));
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ id: result.article.id, draw: result.draw }));
  `;
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['--input-type=module', '-e', program], {
    encoding: 'utf8',
    stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    env: {
      ...process.env,
      PROGRESS_PATH: progressPath,
      RESERVATION_ID: reservationId,
      RECORD_PATH: recordPath,
      CANDIDATES: JSON.stringify(candidates),
      HOLD_AFTER_RESERVE: holdAfterReserve ? '1' : '0',
      READY_PATH: readyPath,
    },
  });
  let stdout = '';
  let stderr = '';
  child.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => { stdout += chunk.toString(); });
  child.stderr.on('data', (chunk) => { stderr += chunk.toString(); });
  const closed = new Promise((resolveClose) => child.once('close', (code, signal) => resolveClose({ code, signal, stdout, stderr })));
  return { child, closed };
}

afterEach(async () => {
  if (temporaryRoot) await rm(temporaryRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
  temporaryRoot = null;
});

describe('global article reservations', () => {
  test('two real worker processes cannot reserve the same article', async () => {
    temporaryRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-article-race-'));
    const progressPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'global-article-progress.json');
    const candidates = fixtureCollection().candidates;
    const first = startReservationProcess({
      progressPath, reservationId: 'run-a::iteration-001', recordPath: join(temporaryRoot, 'a.json'), candidates,
    });
    const second = startReservationProcess({
      progressPath, reservationId: 'run-b::iteration-001', recordPath: join(temporaryRoot, 'b.json'), candidates,
    });
    const results = await Promise.all([first.closed, second.closed]);
    expect(results.map(({ code }) => code)).toEqual([0, 0]);
    const selected = results.map(({ stdout }) => JSON.parse(stdout).id);
    expect(new Set(selected).size).toBe(2);
    const progress = JSON.parse(await readFile(progressPath, 'utf8'));
    expect(Object.values(progress.reservations).map(({ article }) => article.id).sort()).toEqual([...selected].sort());
    expect(progress.seenArticleKeys).toEqual([]);
  });

  test('releases a crashed pre-record reservation and eventually returns its article to the queue', async () => {
    temporaryRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-article-recovery-'));
    const progressPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'global-article-progress.json');
    const collection = fixtureCollection();
    const crashedRecordPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'crashed', 'record.json');
    const crashedReadyPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'crashed-owner-ready');
    const crashedReservation = startReservationProcess({
      progressPath,
      reservationId: 'run-crashed::iteration-001',
      recordPath: crashedRecordPath,
      candidates: collection.candidates,
      holdAfterReserve: true,
      readyPath: crashedReadyPath,
    });
    const crashDeadline = Date.now() + 5000;
    while (Date.now() < crashDeadline) {
      try { await access(crashedReadyPath); break; } catch {}
      if (crashedReservation.child.exitCode !== null) throw new Error('Reservation owner exited before it could be crashed');
      await new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, 10));
    }
    await access(crashedReadyPath);
    crashedReservation.child.kill('SIGKILL');
    const crashedResult = await crashedReservation.closed;
    expect(crashedResult.signal).toBe('SIGKILL');

    const commit = async (reservation, reservationId, recordPath) => {
      await mkdir(dirname(recordPath), { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(recordPath, JSON.stringify({ article: reservation.article }));
      return commitGlobalArticleReservation({
        progressPath,
        articleProgress: reservation.articleProgress,
        reservationId,
        article: reservation.article,
        selection: {
          sourceAggregate: collection.aggregatePath,
          aggregateHash: collection.aggregateHash,
          draw: reservation.draw,
        },
      });
    };

    const second = await reserveGlobalArticle({
      progressPath,
      collection,
      reservationId: 'run-b::iteration-001',
      recordPath: join(temporaryRoot, 'b', 'record.json'),
      hasUsableArticleImage: async () => true,
    });
    expect(second.article.id).toBe('article-2');
    await commit(second, 'run-b::iteration-001', join(temporaryRoot, 'b', 'record.json'));

    const third = await reserveGlobalArticle({
      progressPath,
      collection,
      reservationId: 'run-c::iteration-001',
      recordPath: join(temporaryRoot, 'c', 'record.json'),
      hasUsableArticleImage: async () => true,
    });
    expect(third.article.id).toBe('article-3');
    await commit(third, 'run-c::iteration-001', join(temporaryRoot, 'c', 'record.json'));

    const fourth = await reserveGlobalArticle({
      progressPath,
      collection,
      reservationId: 'run-d::iteration-001',
      recordPath: join(temporaryRoot, 'd', 'record.json'),
      hasUsableArticleImage: async () => true,
    });
    expect(fourth.article.id).toBe('article-1');
    expect(articleProgressSnapshot(fourth.articleProgress).seenArticleKeys).toHaveLength(2);
    await expect(access(crashedRecordPath)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ENOENT' });
  });

  test('recovers a persisted record as committed after a crash before history commit', async () => {
    temporaryRoot = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-article-commit-'));
    const progressPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'global-article-progress.json');
    const collection = fixtureCollection();
    const recordPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'run', 'iteration-001', 'record.json');
    await mkdir(join(temporaryRoot, 'run', 'iteration-001'), { recursive: true });
    const ownerReadyPath = join(temporaryRoot, 'owner-ready');
    const initialProcess = startReservationProcess({
      progressPath, reservationId: 'run::iteration-001', recordPath, candidates: collection.candidates,
      holdAfterReserve: true, readyPath: ownerReadyPath,
    });
    const crashDeadline = Date.now() + 5000;
    while (Date.now() < crashDeadline) {
      try { await access(ownerReadyPath); break; } catch {}
      if (initialProcess.child.exitCode !== null) throw new Error('Reservation owner exited before it could be crashed');
      await new Promise((resolveDelay) => setTimeout(resolveDelay, 10));
    }
    await access(ownerReadyPath);
    initialProcess.child.kill('SIGKILL');
    expect((await initialProcess.closed).signal).toBe('SIGKILL');
    await mkdir(dirname(recordPath), { recursive: true });
    await writeFile(recordPath, JSON.stringify({ article: collection.candidates[0].article }));

    const next = await reserveGlobalArticle({
      progressPath, collection, reservationId: 'next::iteration-001', recordPath: join(temporaryRoot, 'next.json'),
      hasUsableArticleImage: async () => true,
    });
    expect(next.article.id).toBe('article-2');
    expect(next.articleProgress.seenArticleKeys).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(next.articleProgress.reservations['run::iteration-001'].status).toBe('committed');
  });
});

import { describe, expect, test } from '@jest/globals';
import { advanceWorkerSchedule, createInitialWorkerSchedule, recoverWorkerSchedule } from '../../eu-reski-news/news-scene/parallel-schedule.mjs';

describe('persistent parallel worker schedules', () => {
  test('derives independent 10/hour and 5/hour spacing from a common hour window', () => {
    const auto = createInitialWorkerSchedule(10_000, 360_000, 'on');
    const manual = createInitialWorkerSchedule(10_000, 720_000, 'on');
    expect(auto.nextStartAtMs).toBe(190_000);
    expect(manual.nextStartAtMs).toBe(370_000);
    expect(advanceWorkerSchedule(auto, 10_000, 360_000).schedule.nextStartAtMs).toBe(550_000);
    expect(advanceWorkerSchedule(manual, 10_000, 720_000).schedule.nextStartAtMs).toBe(1_090_000);
  });

  test('never catches up missed starts after restart or a long generation', () => {
    const recovered = recoverWorkerSchedule({ lastStartAtMs: 10_000, nextStartAtMs: 370_000 }, 1_000_000, 360_000);
    expect(recovered.nextStartAtMs).toBe(1_360_000);

    const afterLongGeneration = advanceWorkerSchedule({ nextStartAtMs: 370_000 }, 1_000_000, 360_000);
    expect(afterLongGeneration.startAtMs).toBe(1_000_000);
    expect(afterLongGeneration.schedule.nextStartAtMs).toBe(1_360_000);
  });

  test('preserves a future scheduled slot and repairs invalid saved state safely', () => {
    expect(recoverWorkerSchedule({ nextStartAtMs: 2_000 }, 1_000, 60_000).nextStartAtMs).toBe(2_000);
    expect(recoverWorkerSchedule({}, 1_000, 60_000).nextStartAtMs).toBe(61_000);
    expect(recoverWorkerSchedule({ scenePath: '/scene', intervalMs: 60_000 }, 1_000, 60_000))
      .toMatchObject({ scenePath: '/scene', intervalMs: 60_000, nextStartAtMs: 61_000 });
    expect(advanceWorkerSchedule({ scenePath: '/scene', nextStartAtMs: 2_000 }, 1_000, 60_000).schedule)
      .toMatchObject({ scenePath: '/scene', nextStartAtMs: 62_000 });
  });
});

import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';

const runnerPath = fileURLToPath(new URL('../../eu-reski-news/scripts/run-news-scene-image-iteration.mjs', import.meta.url));

describe('parallel worker pending media resume', () => {
  test('polls same saved task across restart, creates no new iteration or article reservation', async () => {
    const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'eu-reski-pending-media-'));
    const outputDirectory = join(root, 'output');
    const runDirectory = join(outputDirectory, 'run-001');
    const iterationDirectory = join(runDirectory, 'iteration-001');
    const mediaDirectory = join(iterationDirectory, 'media');
    const semanticSequence = join(root, 'semantic-sequence.json');
    const pollScript = join(root, 'fake-video-poll.mjs');
    const pollLog = join(root, 'poll-log.json');
    const taskId = 'provider-task-original-123';
    try {
      await mkdir(mediaDirectory, { recursive: true });
      await writeFile(semanticSequence, JSON.stringify([{ provider: 'offline', capturedAt: '2026-10-28T12:00:00.000Z', words: [], entries: [{ id: 'saved', seed: ['offline', 'test'], item: { title: 'saved' } }] }]));
      const savedSemantic = {
        provider: 'offline',
        capturedAt: '2026-10-28T12:00:00.000Z',
        words: [],
        entries: [{ id: 'saved', seed: ['offline', 'test'], item: { title: 'saved' } }],
      };
      await writeFile(join(iterationDirectory, 'record.json'), JSON.stringify({
        status: 'Abgeschlossen',
        autoMode: true,
        sceneCount: 1,
        autoDecision: { promptVersion: 'eu-reski-auto-director-v2', sceneCount: 1, sceneCountReason: 'One scene for fixture.' },
        semantic: savedSemantic,
        filmPlan: { scenePlan: [{ index: 1, videoMode: 'image_to_video', videoTaskUUID: taskId }] },
        videoResults: [{ status: 'processing', taskUUID: taskId }],
      }));
      await writeFile(join(runDirectory, 'run.json'), JSON.stringify({
        startedAt: '2026-10-28T11:00:00.000Z',
        iterationLimit: 1,
        status: 'pending_media',
        iterations: [{ index: 1, directory: 'iteration-001', report: 'iteration-001/index.html' }],
        articleProgress: null,
        runOptions: {
          'auto-mode': true,
          'article-order': 'global',
          'videos-after-each-iteration': true,
          'prompt-only': true,
          'stream-sequence-file': semanticSequence,
          'no-style-reference': true,
          'no-guernica-figure-reference': true,
        },
      }));
      await writeFile(pollScript, [
        "import { appendFile, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';",
        "import { join } from 'node:path';",
        'const value = (prefix) => process.argv.find((argument) => argument.startsWith(prefix))?.slice(prefix.length);',
        'const run = value("--run="); const iteration = value("--iteration=");',
        'const recordPath = join(run, iteration, "record.json");',
        'const record = JSON.parse(await readFile(recordPath, "utf8"));',
        'const taskUUID = record.videoResults[0].taskUUID;',
        'await appendFile(process.env.TEST_POLL_LOG, `${taskUUID}\\n`);',
        'if (Number((await readFile(process.env.TEST_POLL_COUNT, "utf8").catch(() => "0"))) > 0) {',
        '  await mkdir(join(run, iteration, "media"), { recursive: true });',
        '  await writeFile(join(run, iteration, "media", "scene-1-video.mp4"), "offline-video");',
        '}',
        'const count = Number(await readFile(process.env.TEST_POLL_COUNT, "utf8").catch(() => "0")) + 1;',
        'await writeFile(process.env.TEST_POLL_COUNT, String(count));',
      ].join('\n'));

      const runResume = () => spawnSync(process.execPath, [
        runnerPath,
        `--resume-run=${runDirectory}`,
        `--output-dir=${outputDirectory}`,
        '--iterations=1',
      ], {
        encoding: 'utf8',
        timeout: 15000,
        env: {
          ...process.env,
          OPENAI_API_KEY: 'offline-test-key',
          NEWS_TEST_VIDEO_POLL_SCRIPT: pollScript,
          TEST_POLL_LOG: pollLog,
          TEST_POLL_COUNT: join(root, 'poll-count'),
        },
      });

      const first = runResume();
      if (first.status !== 0) throw new Error(`${first.stdout}\n${first.stderr}`);
      expect(first.status).toBe(0);
      let manifest = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(runDirectory, 'run.json'), 'utf8'));
      expect(manifest.status).toBe('pending_media');
      expect(manifest.iterations).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(await readFile(pollLog, 'utf8')).toBe(`${taskId}\n`);

      const second = runResume();
      if (second.status !== 0) throw new Error(`${second.stdout}\n${second.stderr}`);
      expect(second.status).toBe(0);
      manifest = JSON.parse(await readFile(join(runDirectory, 'run.json'), 'utf8'));
      expect(manifest.status).toBe('completed');
      expect(manifest.iterations).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(await readFile(pollLog, 'utf8')).toBe(`${taskId}\n${taskId}\n`);
      expect(JSON.parse(await readFile(join(iterationDirectory, 'record.json'), 'utf8')).videoResults[0].taskUUID).toBe(taskId);
      expect(await readFile(join(mediaDirectory, 'scene-1-video.mp4'), 'utf8')).toBe('offline-video');
      await expect(readFile(join(outputDirectory, 'global-article-progress.json'), 'utf8')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ENOENT' });
      await expect(readFile(join(iterationDirectory, 'auto-director-request.json'), 'utf8')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ENOENT' });
    } finally {
      await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });
});

export const DEFAULT_SCENE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = [
  'You create short visual scene plans for image-to-video generation.',
  'Return only valid JSON.',
  'Build one simple story arc, but make every scene visibly different from the previous scene.',
  'Source cues are the semantic story anchors for the plan. They must drive the story, not sit in the background as optional inspiration.',
  'Treat source cues as an ordered wordstream. The sequence and adjacency of cue words should visibly shift scene state over time.',
  'Every scene must clearly reflect the source cues through title, beat, storyBeat, stillPrompt, imageDescription, and prompt wording.',
  'When source cues are genres, titles, keywords, or fragments rather than a full synopsis, infer a coherent micro-story from them and make that inferred story visible across the sequence.',
  'Turn the source cues into embodied, visible scene events rather than commentary, taxonomy, explanation, or topic-summary.',
  'For every scene after the first, carry a visible residue of the previous scene into the new one through expression, posture, room pressure, aftermath, or transformed setup.',
  'Do not default to a generic presenter, explainer, interview, or host-discussion structure unless the source cues explicitly describe that format.',
  'Prefer titles, beats, and storyBeat text built from concrete visible action, emotional pressure, framing change, and physical behavior.',

  'Adjacent scenes must not reuse the same dominant beat skeleton, motion skeleton, or camera skeleton unless the cue progression itself clearly repeats.',
  'For scenes after the first, stillPrompt must describe the next destination image the shot should arrive at, not a paraphrase of the previous frame.',
  'Change at least the setting, primary action, or framing emphasis in every next scene.',

  'stillPrompt must describe one still image.',

  'videoMode must be either "firstLast" or "singleImage".',
  'durationSeconds must be the real clip duration for that scene in seconds.',
  'The sum of all durationSeconds values should match the total requested film duration as closely as possible.',

  'singleImagePrompt must be a final ready-to-use prompt for single-image video mode, not notes or instructions.',

  'Each video prompt must focus on visible action, scene change, and one camera move.',
  'Do not write meta instructions, labels, bullet points, or explanations inside the prompt fields.',
  'Do not mention JSON, schema, scene object, first sentence, second sentence, or any prompt-writing instructions inside the prompt fields.',
  'If videoMode is "firstLast", videoPrompt must clearly move from the current frame toward the destination scene.',
  'If videoMode is "singleImage", singleImagePrompt must animate the current frame with believable motion without requiring a generated end frame.',
  'storyBeat must explain what new moment this scene adds.',
  'motionCue and cameraCue must be short and concrete.',
  'freshImage must match the setup choice: true when a new image is needed, false when the previous last frame should be reused.',

  'When configMode is "camera", if the source cues imply unseen objects or events, express them through reaction, posture, gaze, facial change, framing, or lighting emphasis instead of inserting the unseen object into the shot.',
  'When configMode is "camera", make every scene feel physically readable and cinematically charged; avoid abstract or essay-like beats when a bodily or framing-based change can carry the same idea.',

            },
            durationSeconds: { type: 'number' },

            freshImage: { type: 'boolean' },
            useCameraShot: { type: 'boolean' },
          },
        },
      },
    },
  },
  strict: true,
};

export const resolveSceneCountFromConfig = ({
  sceneLengths = [],
  sceneCount,
  defaultSceneCount = 1,
} = {}) => {
  const explicitCount = Number(sceneCount);
  if (Number.isFinite(explicitCount) && explicitCount > 0) {
    return Math.floor(explicitCount);
  }

  if (typeof sceneLengths === 'function') {
    return clampSceneCount(defaultSceneCount);
  }

  const derivedCount = Array.isArray(sceneLengths)
    ? sceneLengths.filter((value) => Number.isFinite(Number(value)) && Number(value) > 0).length
    : 0;

  if (derivedCount > 0) {
    return derivedCount;
  }

  return clampSceneCount(defaultSceneCount);
};

export const resolveSceneLengthsInput = async (sceneLengths, sceneCount, fallbackLength = 3) => {
  const count = clampSceneCount(sceneCount);

  if (typeof sceneLengths === 'function') {
    const resolved = [];
    for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) {
      resolved.push(normalizeSceneLengthValue(await sceneLengths({ index, count }), fallbackLength));
    }
    return resolved;
  }

  if (Array.isArray(sceneLengths)) {
    const normalized = sceneLengths
      .map((value) => normalizeSceneLengthValue(value, fallbackLength))
      .filter((value) => Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0);

    if (normalized.length >= count) {
      return normalized.slice(0, count);
    }

    while (normalized.length < count) {
      normalized.push(fallbackLength);
    }
    return normalized;
  }

  return Array.from({ length: count }, () => fallbackLength);
};

export const parseScenePlan = (value, sceneCount = 3, resolvedSceneLengths = []) => {
  const count = clampSceneCount(sceneCount);
  const parsed = JSON.parse(stripCodeFences(value));
  if (!Array.isArray(parsed?.scenes)) {
    throw new Error('Scene plan response must be an object with a scenes array');
  }

  return parsed.scenes.slice(0, count).map((item, index) => ({
    index: index + 1,
    title: normalizeString(item?.title, `Scene ${index + 1}`),
    beat: normalizeString(item?.beat, ''),
    stillPrompt: normalizeString(item?.stillPrompt, ''),
    imageDescription: normalizeString(item?.imageDescription, ''),
    storyBeat: normalizeString(item?.storyBeat, ''),
    motionCue: normalizeString(item?.motionCue, ''),
    cameraCue: normalizeString(item?.cameraCue, ''),
    frameSource: normalizeFrameSource(item?.frameSource, item?.freshImage === true ? 'newImage' : 'lastFrame'),
    videoMode: normalizeVideoMode(item?.videoMode, 'singleImage'),
    durationSeconds: normalizeSceneLengthValue(
      resolvedSceneLengths[index],
      normalizeSceneLengthValue(item?.durationSeconds, 3)
    ),
    videoPrompt: normalizeString(item?.videoPrompt, ''),
    singleImagePrompt: normalizeString(item?.singleImagePrompt, ''),
    freshImage: item?.freshImage === true,
    useCameraShot: item?.useCameraShot === true,
  }));
};

export const parseScenePlanLengthMismatch = (error) => {
  const message = String(error?.message || error || '');
  const match = message.match(/Scene plan length mismatch: expected (\d+), received (\d+)/i);
  if (!match) {
    return null;
  }

  return {
    expected: Number(match[1]),
    received: Number(match[2]),
  };
};

export const generateScenePlanWithFallback = async ({
  generateScenes,
  sceneCount,
  sceneLengths,
  configMode,
  sceneFlavor,
  visualDirection,
  visionStoryContext,
  sourceCues,
  onFallback,
} = {}) => {
  if (typeof generateScenes !== 'function') {
    throw new Error('generateScenePlanWithFallback requires a generateScenes function');
  }

  let targetSceneCount = clampSceneCount(sceneCount);

  while (targetSceneCount >= 1) {
    try {
      const activeSceneLengths = Array.isArray(sceneLengths)
        ? sceneLengths.slice(0, targetSceneCount)
        : await resolveSceneLengthsInput(sceneLengths, targetSceneCount, 3);
      const scenePlan = await generateScenes({
        sceneCount: targetSceneCount,
        sceneLengths: activeSceneLengths,
        configMode,
        sceneFlavor,
        visualDirection,
        visionStoryContext,
        sourceCues,
      });

      return {
        scenePlan,
        effectiveSceneCount: targetSceneCount,
        effectiveSceneLengths: activeSceneLengths,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      const mismatch = parseScenePlanLengthMismatch(error);
      if (!mismatch) {
        throw error;
      }

      const nextSceneCount = Math.max(1, Math.min(targetSceneCount - 1, mismatch.received));
      if (nextSceneCount >= targetSceneCount) {
        throw error;
      }

      if (typeof onFallback === 'function') {
        await onFallback({
          requestedSceneCount: targetSceneCount,
          receivedSceneCount: mismatch.received,
          nextSceneCount,
          mismatch,
          error,
        });
      }

      targetSceneCount = nextSceneCount;
    }
  }

  throw new Error('Unable to generate a valid scene plan.');
};

export const createSceneGenerator = ({
  openai,
  fallbackOpenai,
  model,
  fallbackModel = '',
  systemPrompt = '',
  temperature = 0.4,
  top_p = 0.9,
} = {}) => {
  if (!openai?.chat?.completions?.create) {
    throw new Error('createSceneGenerator requires an OpenAI-compatible client');
  }

  const resolvedSystemPrompt = normalizeString(systemPrompt, DEFAULT_SCENE_SYSTEM_PROMPT);

  return async ({
    sourceCues = [],
    sceneLengths = [],
    sceneCount = 3,
    visualDirection = '',
    visionStoryContext = '',
    configMode = 'generated',
    sceneFlavor = 'default',
  } = {}) => {
    const count = clampSceneCount(sceneCount);
    const resolvedSceneLengths = await resolveSceneLengthsInput(sceneLengths, count, 3);
    const trimmedCues = sourceCues
      .map((cue) => normalizeString(cue))
      .filter(Boolean)
      .slice(0, count);
    const normalizedVisionStoryContext = normalizeString(visionStoryContext);

    const messages = [
      {
        role: 'system',
        content: resolvedSystemPrompt,
      },
      {
        role: 'user',
        content: [
          `Scene count: ${count}`,
          `Scene lengths (seconds): ${resolvedSceneLengths.join(', ') || '3'}`,
          `Total film duration (seconds): ${resolvedSceneLengths.reduce((sum, value) => sum + Number(value || 0), 0) || 3}`,
          `Config mode: ${normalizeString(configMode, 'generated')}`,
          `Scene flavor: ${normalizeString(sceneFlavor, 'default')}`,
          `Visual direction: ${normalizeString(visualDirection, 'documentary, realistic, concise')}`,
          `Source cues (ordered semantic story anchors): ${JSON.stringify(trimmedCues)}`,
          'Wordstream rule: treat source cues as an ordered wordstream and map each next cue into a visible scene-state shift.',
          'Story requirement: treat the source cues as the story spine. Build a coherent micro-story that starts from the early cues and develops, escalates, or transforms through the later cues.',
          'Story progression rule: scenes must feel causally linked; each next scene should happen because of the previous one, not as a disconnected new label.',
          'Scene memory rule: after scene 1, let the previous scene leave a visible residue in the next one through expression, posture, room tension, visible aftermath, or a changed setup.',
          'If the source cues are genres, titles, or keywords, infer concrete visual story beats from them instead of falling back to a generic host, explainer, or discussion format.',
          'Prefer embodied scene beats with visible stakes, gesture, posture, expression, framing pressure, or environmental tension over abstract explanation or topic-summary wording.',
          'Action rule: every beat and storyBeat must contain a decisive visible change or action, not just a mood label or static description.',
          'Motion rule: motionCue must describe what physically changes in the shot. Do not use framing descriptions or static close-up wording as a motionCue.',
          'Camera rule: cameraCue must describe one readable camera behavior that intensifies the scene event rather than merely restating framing.',
          'Interaction rule: each cue must visibly act on the subject, the room, or both together; make that relationship explicit instead of leaving the cue unattached to the shot.',
          'Interaction variety rule: across the sequence, vary whether the cue mainly changes the body, the room emphasis, or both at once.',
          'Curious spectacle rule: use the screenshot and the cue words to stage something visibly strange, charged, or inquisitive inside the same shot instead of flattening the cue into neutral coverage.',
          'Adjacent scenes must not repeat the same dominant beat skeleton, motion pattern, or camera pattern unless the cue progression itself repeats.',
          'Wordstream visual mapping: express cue changes through location emphasis (which part of the room/background dominates), actor movement/body language, and sensory visual description (light, texture, atmosphere, tension).',
          'Cue consequence rule: each cue must change beat and storyBeat, and must also change at least one of motionCue, cameraCue, stillPrompt, or imageDescription in a visible way.',
          'If cue text is fragmentary, infer a concrete visible consequence in the shot instead of falling back to generic placeholders like reflection, discussion, or vague tension.',
          `Trippy flavor rule: ${
            normalizeString(sceneFlavor, 'default').toLowerCase() === 'ltxtrippy'
              ? 'allow the cue words to create surreal visible events, hallucinated objects, warped room geometry, transformed light, symbolic props, and uncanny action inside the shot. Do not limit the plan to slight gaze or posture changes. At least half of the scenes should contain a strong visible transformation or event.'
              : 'n/a'
          }`,
          `Trippy action rule: ${
            normalizeString(sceneFlavor, 'default').toLowerCase() === 'ltxtrippy'
              ? 'for ltxTrippy, beats should read like scene events: something appears, spreads, bends, bursts, swallows, floods, circles, melts, multiplies, or overtakes the room or subject.'
              : 'n/a'
          }`,
          normalizedVisionStoryContext
            ? `Vision story context from the source shot: ${normalizedVisionStoryContext}`
            : 'Vision story context from the source shot: n/a',
          normalizedVisionStoryContext
            ? 'If vision story context is provided, use it as continuity for each scene, especially location, actors, and visible-shot description.'
            : 'If vision story context is provided, use it as continuity for each scene.',
          `Camera-mode reminder: ${
            normalizeString(configMode, 'generated') === 'camera'
              ? 'keep the story inside the real visible shot, but still let the source cues control the emotional arc, tension, and implied events.'
              : 'n/a'
          }`,
          `Camera-mode visible-anchor rule: ${
            normalizeString(configMode, 'generated') === 'camera'
              ? 'use the vision story context as the source of truth for location, actor identity, and visible scene setup. Build stillPrompt, imageDescription, motionCue, cameraCue, videoPrompt, and singleImagePrompt from that visible basis instead of inventing unsupported elements.'
              : 'n/a'
          }`,
          `Camera-mode firstLast rule: ${
            normalizeString(configMode, 'generated') === 'camera'
              ? 'use firstLast only for small believable changes between start and destination inside the same visible room, such as pose, gaze, expression, crop, or lighting emphasis. Do not use firstLast to reveal new objects or widen into a new setup.'
              : 'n/a'
          }`,
        ].join('\n'),
      },
    ];

    const requestScenePlan = async (client, activeModel) => client.chat.completions.create({
      model: activeModel,
      messages,
      response_format: { type: 'json_schema', json_schema: SCENE_PLAN_SCHEMA },
      temperature,
      top_p,
    });

    let response;
    try {
      response = await requestScenePlan(openai, model);
    } catch (primaryError) {
      if (!fallbackOpenai?.chat?.completions?.create) {
        throw primaryError;
      }

      try {
        response = await requestScenePlan(fallbackOpenai, fallbackModel || model);
      } catch (fallbackError) {
        throw new Error(
          `Scene plan request failed with primary model "${model}": ${primaryError.message}; fallback model "${fallbackModel || model}": ${fallbackError.message}`
        );
      }
    }

    const content = response?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content;
    const scenePlan = parseScenePlan(content, count, resolvedSceneLengths);

    if (scenePlan.length !== count) {
      const error = new Error(`Scene plan length mismatch: expected ${count}, received ${scenePlan.length}`);
      error.scenePlan = scenePlan;
      error.resolvedSceneLengths = resolvedSceneLengths.slice(0, scenePlan.length);
      throw error;
    }

    return scenePlan;
  };
};

export const getScenePlanEntry = (scenePlan, sceneContext = {}) => {
  if (!Array.isArray(scenePlan) || scenePlan.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }
  const index = Math.max(0, Number(sceneContext?.index || 1) - 1);
  return scenePlan[index] || null;
};

export const compactScenePrompt = (value, maxSentences = 3, maxWords = 55) => {
  const normalized = normalizeString(value).replace(/\s*[\r\n]+\s*/g, ' ').trim();
  if (!normalized) {
    return normalized;
  }

  const sentenceParts = normalized
    .split(/(?<=[.!?])\s+/)
    .map((part) => part.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, maxSentences);

  const compact = sentenceParts.join(' ');
  const truncatedWords = compact.split(' ').filter(Boolean).slice(0, maxWords).join(' ').trim();
  if (!truncatedWords) {
    return truncatedWords;
  }
  if (/[.!?]$/.test(truncatedWords)) {
    return truncatedWords;
  }

  const lastSentenceStart = Math.max(
    truncatedWords.lastIndexOf('. '),
    truncatedWords.lastIndexOf('! '),
    truncatedWords.lastIndexOf('? ')
  );
  if (lastSentenceStart > 0) {
    return `${truncatedWords.slice(0, lastSentenceStart + 1).trim()}`;
  }
  return `${truncatedWords}.`;
};

export const buildFallbackStillPrompt = (sourcePrompt) => {
  const cue = normalizeString(sourcePrompt, 'a simple real-world subject');
  if (/freshweb documentary still of/i.test(cue) || /\bdocumentary still\b/i.test(cue)) {
    return cue;
  }
  return `freshweb documentary still of ${cue}, natural light, candid framing, realistic detail`;
};

export const buildFallbackVideoPrompt = (scenePlanEntry, fallbackText) => compactScenePrompt(
  `${normalizeString(scenePlanEntry?.storyBeat || scenePlanEntry?.beat, fallbackText)} ${normalizeString(scenePlanEntry?.motionCue)} ${normalizeString(scenePlanEntry?.cameraCue)}`
);
